function getAvailableYears(){ var years = Array.from(new Set((state.months||[]).map(getMonthYear).filter(Boolean))); // Ajouter uniquement les ann√©es r√©ellement pr√©sentes dans les onglets du tableur. if(typeof window.FinanceSheet !== "undefined" && window.FinanceSheet){ try{ var snap = window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot ? window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot() : null; if(snap && snap.sheets){ snap.sheets.forEach(function(sh){ var name = String(sh.name||"").trim(); var m = name.match(/(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})/); if(m && years.indexOf(m[0]) === -1) years.push(m[0]); }); } }catch(e){} } return Array.from(new Set(years)).sort(sortYearValues); } function chooseDefaultYear(years,months,preferredYear="",preferredMonthId=""){ const list=(years||[]).filter(Boolean); if(!list.length) return ""; if(preferredYear&&list.includes(preferredYear)) return preferredYear; // Les ann√©es vides restent s√©lectionnables, sans devenir le choix initial // au d√©triment de l'ann√©e qui contient r√©ellement les donn√©es import√©es. const withData=Array.from(new Set((months||[]).map(getMonthYear).filter(Boolean))).sort(sortYearValues); const current=String(new Date().getFullYear()); if(withData.includes(current)) return current; if(withData.length) return withData[0]; return list.includes(current)?current:list.sort(sortYearValues)[0]||""; } function getVisibleMonths(){ const years=getAvailableYears(); if(!years.length) return []; if(!state.selectedYear||!years.includes(state.selectedYear)) state.selectedYear=chooseDefaultYear(years,state.months,state.selectedYear,state.selectedMonthId); return (state.months||[]).filter(month=>getMonthYear(month)===state.selectedYear); } function populateYearSelect(){ if(!elements.yearSelect) return; const years=getAvailableYears(); elements.yearSelect.innerHTML=""; if(!years.length){ const opt=document.createElement("option"); opt.value=""; opt.textContent="Aucune ann√©e disponible"; elements.yearSelect.appendChild(opt); elements.yearSelect.value=""; elements.yearSelect.disabled=true; state.selectedYear=""; syncCustomDropdown(elements.yearSelect); return; } state.selectedYear=chooseDefaultYear(years,state.months,state.selectedYear,state.selectedMonthId); years.forEach(year=>{ const opt=document.createElement("option"); opt.value=year; opt.textContent=getYearLabel(year); elements.yearSelect.appendChild(opt); }); elements.yearSelect.value=state.selectedYear; elements.yearSelect.disabled=false; syncCustomDropdown(elements.yearSelect); } function populateMonthSelect(){ populateYearSelect(); const months=getVisibleMonths(); elements.monthSelect.innerHTML=""; if(!months.length){ const opt=document.createElement("option"); opt.value=""; opt.textContent="Aucun mois disponible"; elements.monthSelect.appendChild(opt); state.selectedMonthId=""; elements.monthSelect.value=""; elements.monthSelect.disabled=true; syncCustomDropdown(elements.monthSelect); return; } if(!months.some(m=>m.id===state.selectedMonthId)) state.selectedMonthId=months[months.length-1]?.id||""; months.forEach(m=>{ const opt=document.createElement("option"); opt.value=m.id; opt.textContent=m.label; elements.monthSelect.appendChild(opt); }); elements.monthSelect.value=state.selectedMonthId; elements.monthSelect.disabled=false; syncCustomDropdown(elements.monthSelect); } function updateInterface(){ populateMonthSelect(); const hasData=getVisibleMonths().length>0; elements.emptyState.classList.toggle("is-visible",!hasData); if(!hasData) elements.emptyState.textContent=state.selectedYear?`Aucun mois renseign√© pour ${getYearLabel(state.selectedYear)}.`:"Aucune donn√©e √† afficher pour le moment."; if(elements.yearSelect) syncCustomDropdown(elements.yearSelect); syncCustomDropdown(elements.monthSelect); updateSummaryCards(); updateMonthDetails(getSelectedMonth()); renderCategorySettings(); renderCustomRulesList(); renderChart(); } function updateMonthDetails(selected){ elements.savingsBreakdown.innerHTML=""; const visibleMonths=getVisibleMonths(); const items=!selected||state.isGlobal?visibleMonths.flatMap(m=>m.ruleBreakdown||[]):(selected.ruleBreakdown||[]); const visible=items.filter(i=>i.value!==0); const rows=visible.length?visible:[{name:"-",value:0,color:COLORS.tick}]; rows.forEach(i=>{ elements.savingsBreakdown.appendChild(createDetailRow(i.name,formatEuro(i.value),i.color||getRuleColor(i.name))); }); const allRules=visibleMonths.length?formatRuleLines(visibleMonths.flatMap(m=>m.ruleBreakdown||[])):"-"; elements.bigExpenses.textContent=selected&&selected.bigExpenses!=="-"?`${selected.bigExpenses}`:allRules; } function formatRuleLines(items){ const m=new Map(); items.forEach(i=>{ if(i&&i.name) m.set(i.name,(m.get(i.name)||0)+Number(i.value||0)); }); return Array.from(m.entries()).filter(([,v])=>v!==0).map(([name,val])=>`${window.GFI18n?window.GFI18n.t(name):name}: ${formatEuro(val)}`).join("\n")||"-"; } function createDetailRow(lbl,val,col){ const row=document.createElement("div"); row.className="detail-row"; const name=document.createElement("span"); name.textContent=["Salaire","D√©pense","√âpargne"].includes(lbl)&&window.GFI18n?window.GFI18n.t(lbl):lbl; name.style.color=col; const amount=document.createElement("strong"); amount.textContent=val; row.append(name,amount); return row; } function getSelectedMonth(){ const visible=getVisibleMonths(); return visible.find(m=>m.id===state.selectedMonthId)||visible[visible.length-1]||state.months.find(m=>m.id===state.selectedMonthId); } // ‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê // GRAPHIQUES ‚Äî avec bordure noire sur le camembert // function renderChart(){ if(state.chart){ state.chart.destroy(); state.chart=null; } elements.customLegend.innerHTML=""; const visibleMonths=getVisibleMonths(); if(!visibleMonths.length){ elements.chartTitle.textContent="R√©partition du mois"; elements.chartSubtitle.textContent="Importe une source financi√®re pour afficher les graphiques."; elements.emptyState.classList.add("is-visible"); return; } if(typeof Chart!=="function"){ elements.chartTitle.textContent="Chart.js indisponible"; elements.chartSubtitle.textContent="Le fichier Chart.js n'a pas pu √™tre charg√© depuis le CDN."; elements.emptyState.classList.add("is-visible"); showMessage("Chart.js n'a pas pu √™tre charg√©. V√©rifie ta connexion internet."); return; } const config=getChartConfig(); elements.chartTitle.textContent=config.title; elements.chartSubtitle.textContent=config.subtitle; elements.emptyState.classList.remove("is-visible"); if(window.GFI18n){ const tr=window.GFI18n.t; config.chart.data.labels=(config.chart.data.labels||[]).map(label=>tr(label)); config.chart.data.datasets.forEach(ds=>{ if(["Salaire","D√©pense","D√©penses","√âpargne"].includes(ds.label))ds.label=tr(ds.label); }); } state.chart=new Chart(elements.chartCanvas,config.chart); generateCustomLegend(state.chart); } function generateCustomLegend(chart,target=elements.customLegend){ target.innerHTML=""; const isPie=chart.config.type==="pie"||chart.config.type==="doughnut"; const items=isPie?chart.data.labels.map((lbl,i)=>({text:lbl,color:chart.data.datasets[0].backgroundColor[i],index:i,hidden:!chart.getDataVisibility(i)})):chart.data.datasets.map((ds,i)=>({text:ds.label,color:ds.backgroundColor,index:i,hidden:!chart.isDatasetVisible(i)})); items.forEach(item=>{ const btn=document.createElement("div"); btn.className=`legend-item${item.hidden?" is-hidden":""}`; const swatch=document.createElement("span"); swatch.className="legend-color"; swatch.style.background=item.color; const label=document.createElement("span"); label.textContent=item.text; btn.append(swatch,label); btn.addEventListener("click",()=>{ if(isPie) chart.toggleDataVisibility(item.index); else chart.setDatasetVisibility(item.index,!chart.isDatasetVisible(item.index)); chart.update({duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"}); btn.classList.toggle("is-hidden",isPie?!chart.getDataVisibility(item.index):!chart.isDatasetVisible(item.index)); clearTimeout(chart.__legendRefreshTimer); chart.__legendRefreshTimer=setTimeout(()=>generateCustomLegend(chart,target),600); }); target.appendChild(btn); }); } function getChartConfig(context){ switch(context?.view||state.currentView){ case"grouped":return getGroupedBarConfig(context); case"stacked":return getStackedRulesConfig(context); case"line":return getLineConfig(context); default:return getPieConfig(context); } } function getActiveRules(){ return (state.availableRules?.length?state.availableRules:getAvailableRules()).filter(r=>r!=="Ignorer"&&r!=="Salaire"); } function getMonthRuleValue(month,rule){ return Math.abs(month?.ruleBreakdown?.find(i=>i.name===rule)?.value||0); } function getPieConfig(context){ const visibleMonths=context?context.months:getVisibleMonths(); const month=context?(context.selected||visibleMonths[visibleMonths.length-1]):getSelectedMonth()||visibleMonths[visibleMonths.length-1]||state.months[state.months.length-1]; const items=(month?.ruleBreakdown||[]).filter(i=>i.value!==0); return{ title:`R√©partition - ${month?.label||""}`, subtitle:"Salaire et r√®gles appliqu√©es au mois s√©lectionn√©.", chart:{ type:"doughnut", data:{ labels:["Salaire",...items.map(i=>i.name)], datasets:[{ data:[Math.abs(month?.salary||0),...items.map(i=>Math.abs(i.value))], backgroundColor:[COLORS.salary,...items.map(i=>i.color||getRuleColor(i.name))], borderColor: "#000000", borderWidth: 2, hoverOffset:12 }] }, options:baseChartOptions({ cutout:"60%", layout:{padding:{top:10,bottom:10}}, plugins:{tooltip:tooltipOptions(),legend:{display:false}}, animation:{animateRotate:true,animateScale:true,duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"} }) } }; } function getGroupedBarConfig(context){ const months=context?context.months:getVisibleMonths(); const rules=context?context.rules:getActiveRules(); return{ title:"Comparatif mois par mois", subtitle:"Salaire et r√®gles appliqu√©es sur l‚Äôann√©e s√©lectionn√©e.", chart:{ type:"bar", data:{ labels:months.map(m=>m.label), datasets:[createBarDataset("Salaire",COLORS.salary,months.map(m=>m.salary)),...rules.map(rule=>createBarDataset(rule,getRuleColor(rule),months.map(m=>getMonthRuleValue(m,rule))))] }, options:axisChartOptions({stacked:false,onMonthClick:context?.onMonthClick}) } }; } function getStackedRulesConfig(context){ const months=context?context.months:getVisibleMonths(); const rules=context?context.rules:getActiveRules(); return{ title:"R√©partition par r√®gles", subtitle:"Vue empil√©e de l‚Äôann√©e s√©lectionn√©e.", chart:{ type:"bar", data:{ labels:months.map(m=>m.label), datasets:rules.map(rule=>({ label:rule, data:months.map(m=>getMonthRuleValue(m,rule)), backgroundColor:getRuleColor(rule), borderColor:getRuleColor(rule), borderWidth:1, borderRadius:6, maxBarThickness:44 })) }, options:axisChartOptions({stacked:true,onMonthClick:context?.onMonthClick}) } }; } function getLineConfig(context){ const months=context?context.months:getVisibleMonths(); const rules=context?context.rules:getActiveRules(); return{ title:"√âvolution annuelle", subtitle:"Salaire et r√®gles appliqu√©es sur l‚Äôann√©e s√©lectionn√©e.", chart:{ type:"line", data:{ labels:months.map(m=>m.label), datasets:[createLineDataset("Salaire",COLORS.salary,months.map(m=>m.salary)),...rules.map(rule=>createLineDataset(rule,getRuleColor(rule),months.map(m=>getMonthRuleValue(m,rule))))] }, options:axisChartOptions({stacked:false,onMonthClick:context?.onMonthClick}) } }; } function createBarDataset(l,c,d){ return{label:l,data:d,backgroundColor:c,borderColor:c,borderWidth:1,borderRadius:6,maxBarThickness:36}; } function createLineDataset(l,c,d){ return{label:l,data:d,borderColor:c,backgroundColor:c,pointBackgroundColor:c,pointBorderColor:"#0d1422",pointBorderWidth:2,pointRadius:4,pointHoverRadius:6,borderWidth:3,tension:0.35}; } function axisChartOptions({stacked,onMonthClick}){ return baseChartOptions({ maintainAspectRatio:false, interaction:{mode:"index",intersect:false}, onClick:function(event,active,chart){ const point=(active&&active[0])||chart.getElementsAtEventForMode(event,"nearest",{intersect:true},true)[0]; if(onMonthClick){ if(point) onMonthClick(point.index); return; } const month=getVisibleMonths()[point?.index]; if(month) openChartMonthDetail(month); }, plugins:{tooltip:tooltipOptions(),legend:{display:false}}, scales:{ x:{ stacked, grid:{color:"rgba(255,255,255,0.04)"}, ticks:{color:COLORS.tick,maxRotation:0,autoSkip:true} }, y:{ stacked, beginAtZero:true, grid:{color:COLORS.grid}, ticks:{color:COLORS.tick,callback:v=>compactEuroFormatter.format(v)} } }, animation:{duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"} }); } function baseChartOptions(extra){ return deepMerge({ responsive:true, maintainAspectRatio:false, plugins:{legend:{display:false},tooltip:tooltipOptions()}, transitions:{ active:{animation:{duration:300,easing:"easeOutCubic"}}, show:{animations:{x:{from:0,duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"},y:{from:0,duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"},r:{from:0,duration:1000,easing:"easeOutQuart"},borderWidth:{from:0,duration:500,easing:"linear"}}}, hide:{animations:{x:{to:0,duration:600,easing:"easeInCubic"},y:{to:0,duration:600,easing:"easeInCubic"}}} } },extra); } function tooltipOptions(){ const isDark=document.body.dataset.mode==="dark", theme=document.body.dataset.theme; let bg,text,border; if(theme==="glass"){ bg=isDark?"rgba(6,6,26,0.97)":"rgba(255,255,255,0.97)"; border=isDark?"rgba(129,140,248,0.3)":"rgba(99,102,241,0.25)"; text=isDark?"#e2e8ff":"#1e1b4b"; }else if(theme==="editorial"){ bg=isDark?"rgba(26,18,9,0.97)":"rgba(255,255,255,0.98)"; border=isDark?"rgba(196,168,130,0.3)":"rgba(124,29,29,0.25)"; text=isDark?"#f5f0e8":"#1a1209"; }else{ bg=isDark?"rgba(15,15,15,0.97)":"rgba(255,255,255,0.97)"; border=isDark?"#ffffff":"#0f0f0f"; text=isDark?"#f0ede6":"#0f0f0f"; } return{ backgroundColor:bg, borderColor:border, borderWidth:theme==="brutal"?2:1, titleColor:text, bodyColor:text, padding:12, cornerRadius:theme==="brutal"?0:8, callbacks:{ label:ctx=>{ const lbl=ctx.dataset.label||ctx.label||""; return`${lbl}: ${formatEuro(ctx.parsed?.y??ctx.parsed??0)}`; } } }; } function openChartMonthDetail(month,options={}){ const modal=document.getElementById("chartDetailModal"), title=document.getElementById("chartDetailTitle"), subtitle=document.getElementById("chartDetailSubtitle"), grid=document.getElementById("chartDetailGrid"); if(!modal||!grid) return; title.textContent=`D√©tail ‚Äî ${month.label||"Mois"}`; subtitle.textContent="Clique sur un autre point pour consulter un autre mois."; const lines=[{label:"Salaire",value:month.salary||0},{label:"D√©penses",value:Math.abs(month.expenses||0)},{label:"√âpargne",value:month.savingsTotal||0},...(month.ruleBreakdown||[]).filter(item=>item.name!=="D√©pense"&&item.name!=="√âpargne").map(item=>({label:item.name,value:Math.abs(item.value||0)}))]; } // ‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê // TH√àMES & APPARENCE (avec persistance de la vue) // ‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê function restoreAppearance(){ const savedTheme=safeStore.getItem(STORAGE_KEYS.theme); } function applyAppearance({persist,refreshChart}){ document.body.dataset.theme=state.appearance.theme; document.body.dataset.mode=state.appearance.mode; elements.themeButtons.forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle("is-active",btn.dataset.themeOption===state.appearance.theme)); } function escapeHtml(value){ return String(value ?? "").replace(/[&<>"']/g, ch => ({ "&":"&", "<":"<", ">":">", "\"":""", "'":"'" }[ch])); } function readFileArrayBuffer(file){ return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result); reader.onerror = () => reject(reader.error || new Error("Impossible de lire le fichier")); reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file); }); } function jsonToRows(data){ if(Array.isArray(data)){ if(!data.length) return []; if(Array.isArray(data[0])) return data.map(row => Array.isArray(row) ? row : []); if(data[0] && typeof data[0] === "object"){ const headers = Array.from(new Set(data.flatMap(item => Object.keys(item || {})))); return [headers, ...data.map(item => headers.map(header => item?.[header] ?? ""))]; } return [["Valeur"], ...data.map(item => [item])]; } if(data && typeof data === "object"){ const arrays = Object.values(data).filter(Array.isArray); const preferred = arrays.find(arr => arr.length && typeof arr[0] === "object") || arrays[0]; if(preferred) return jsonToRows(preferred); return [Object.keys(data), Object.values(data)]; } return []; } function xmlToRows(text){ const parser = new DOMParser(); const doc = parser.parseFromString(text, "application/xml"); if(doc.querySelector("parsererror")) return []; const tableNodes = Array.from(doc.getElementsByTagName("table")); if(tableNodes.length){ let bestRows = []; tableNodes.forEach(table => { const rows = Array.from(table.getElementsByTagName("tr")).map(tr => Array.from(tr.children).map(td => safeTrim(td.textContent))); if(rows.length > bestRows.length) bestRows = rows; }); if(bestRows.length) return bestRows; } const root = doc.documentElement; if(!root) return []; const childElements = Array.from(root.children || []); if(!childElements.length) return []; const groupCounts = new Map(); childElements.forEach(el => groupCounts.set(el.tagName, (groupCounts.get(el.tagName) || 0) + 1)); const repeatedTag = Array.from(groupCounts.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0]; if(!repeatedTag || (groupCounts.get(repeatedTag) || 0) < 2) return []; const rows = childElements.filter(el => el.tagName === repeatedTag).map(el => { const cols = Array.from(el.children || []); if(cols.length){ return cols.map(child => safeTrim(child.textContent)); } return [safeTrim(el.textContent)]; }); const headers = Array.from(new Set(rows.flatMap(row => row.map((_, idx) => `Colonne ${idx + 1}`)))); return headers.length ? [headers, ...rows] : rows; } function htmlTableToRows(text){ const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, "text/html"); const tables = Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll("table")); let best = []; tables.forEach(table => { const rows = Array.from(table.rows || []).map(row => Array.from(row.cells || []).map(cell => safeTrim(cell.textContent))); if(rows.length > best.length) best = rows; }); return best; } function scoreHeaderCandidate(row, nextRows){ const stat = rowStats(row); if(!stat.count) return -Infinity; let score = 0; score += stat.strings * 2.1; score += stat.unique * 0.6; score -= stat.numeric * 1.5; score -= stat.dates * 0.9; const joined = row.map(v => safeTrim(v).toLowerCase()).join(" "); const keywordBoosts = ["date", "mois", "month", "ann√©e", "year", "montant", "amount", "salaire", "salary", "montant", "d√©bit", "credit", "transaction", "operation", "op√©ration", "libell√©", "label", "cat√©gorie", "category", "compte", "account"]; if(keywordBoosts.some(k => joined.includes(k))) score += 5.5; if(stat.count <= 2 && stat.strings <= 1) score -= 4; if(nextRows && nextRows.length){ const nextStats = nextRows.map(rowStats); const nextNumeric = nextStats.reduce((a, s) => a + s.numeric, 0) / nextStats.length; const nextDates = nextStats.reduce((a, s) => a + s.dates, 0) / nextStats.length; score += Math.max(0, nextNumeric - stat.numeric) * 0.6; score += Math.max(0, nextDates - stat.dates) * 0.5; } return score; } function looksLikeHeaderRow(row, nextRows){ return scoreHeaderCandidate(row, nextRows) >= 3.5; } function inferColumnType(header, samples){ const dateHits = samples.filter(v => parseLooseDate(v)).length; const numericHits = samples.filter(isNumericLikeValue).length; const textHits = samples.filter(v => !isNumericLikeValue(v) && !parseLooseDate(v) && safeTrim(v) !== "").length; const count = Math.max(1, samples.filter(v => safeTrim(v) !== "").length); const normalized = normalizePropertyName(header); if(/date|mois|month|periode|period|jour|day|transactiondate|datetransaction/.test(normalized) || dateHits / count >= 0.55) return { type: "date", label: "Date" }; if(/ann√©e|annee|year/.test(normalized)) return { type: "number", label: "Ann√©e" }; if(/nom|name|libell|label|description|titre|title|categorie|category|compte|account/.test(normalized) && textHits >= numericHits) return { type: "rich_text", label: "Texte" }; if(numericHits / count >= 0.55) return { type: "number", label: "Num√©rique" }; return { type: "rich_text", label: "Texte" }; } function rowsToImportedPages(rows, fileName, sheetName){ const cleaned = cleanRows(rows); if(!cleaned.length) return []; const analysis = makeAnalysisReport({ name: sheetName || "Feuille 1", rows: cleaned, sourceKind: "table" }, { fileName, sheetName }); return buildPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName); } function resetDataState(){ if(state.chart){ state.chart.destroy(); state.chart = null; } state.rawPages = []; state.months = []; state.detectedProperties = []; state.availableRules = SYSTEM_RULES.slice(); state.selectedMonthId = ""; state.selectedYear = ""; state.isGlobal = false; state.importAnalysis = null; } // ‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê // MESSAGES & UTILITAIRES // ‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê‚ïê function setMessageProgress(progress){ const circle=elements.messageTimerProgress||elements.messageTimer?.querySelector(".message-timer-progress"); } function updateMessagePauseState(paused){ if(!elements.messageBox) return; elements.messageBox.classList.toggle("is-paused",paused); if(elements.messagePauseButton) elements.messagePauseButton.setAttribute("aria-label",paused?"Reprendre l'alerte":"Mettre l'alerte en pause"); messageRafId=null; } } function hideMessage(){ clearMessageTimers(); messagePaused=false; messageDurationMs=15000; messageRemainingMs=15000; messageDeadline=0; if(!elements.messageBox) return; if(messageLeaveTimeoutId){ clearTimeout(messageLeaveTimeoutId); messageLeaveTimeoutId=null; } const finish=()=>{ elements.messageBox.classList.remove("is-visible","is-paused","is-leaving"); if(elements.messageText) elements.messageText.textContent=""; setMessageProgress(0); const rem=Math.max(0,messageDeadline-performance.now()); messageRemainingMs=rem; setMessageProgress(messageDurationMs?rem/messageDurationMs:0); if(rem<=0){ hideMessage(); return; } messageRafId=requestAnimationFrame(tick); }; messageRafId=requestAnimationFrame(tick); messageTimeoutId=setTimeout(hideMessage,messageRemainingMs); } function showMessage(msg,duration=15000){ if(!elements.messageBox) return; clearMessageTimers(); "tableur" : "file"; state.source = active; elements.configTabButtons.forEach(btn => { const isActive = btn.dataset.configTab === active; btn.classList.toggle("active", isActive); btn.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(isActive)); }); elements.configTabPanes.forEach(pane => pane.classList.toggle("active", pane.dataset.configPane === active)); } function getColumnText(column){ return safeTrim(column?.label || `Colonne ${Number(column?.index ?? 0) + 1}`); } function columnSamples(column){ return Array.isArray(column?.samples) ? column.samples : []; } function scoreDateColumn(column){ const label = normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(column)); const samples = columnSamples(column); const dateHits = samples.filter(v => parseLooseDate(v)).length; const count = Math.max(1, samples.filter(v => safeTrim(v) !== "").length); let score = 0; if(/date|mois|month|periode|period|jour|day|transaction|operation|op√©ration|echeance|√©ch√©ance/.test(label)) score += 6; score += (dateHits / count) * 8; if(column?.kind === "date") score += 5; return score; } function scoreAmountColumn(column){ const label = normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(column)); const samples = columnSamples(column); const numericHits = samples.filter(isNumericLikeValue).length; const count = Math.max(1, samples.filter(v => safeTrim(v) !== "").length); let score = 0; if(/montant|amount|valeur|value|debit|d√©bit|credit|cr√©dit|prix|price|total|solde|balance|sum|somme|transaction/.test(label)) score += 6; score += (numericHits / count) * 8; if(column?.kind === "number") score += 4; return score; } function scoreTextColumn(column){ const label = normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(column)); const samples = columnSamples(column); const textHits = samples.filter(v => !isNumericLikeValue(v) && !parseLooseDate(v) && safeTrim(v) !== "").length; const uniqueCount = new Set(samples.map(v => safeTrim(v).toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean)).size; const count = Math.max(1, samples.filter(v => safeTrim(v) !== "").length); let score = 0; if(/categorie|cat√©gorie|category|libelle|libell√©|label|description|details|d√©tails|merchant|beneficiaire|b√©n√©ficiaire|compte|account|moyen|mode/.test(label)) score += 6; score += (textHits / count) * 4; score += Math.min(3, uniqueCount / 8); return score; } function monthKeyFromDate(date){ const d = date instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? date : null; if(!d) return null; return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), 1); } function getRowFirstText(row, preferredIndexes = []){ for(const idx of preferredIndexes){ const val = row?.[idx]; if(safeTrim(val) !== "") return safeTrim(val); } for(const val of (Array.isArray(row) ? row : [])){ if(safeTrim(val) !== "") return safeTrim(val); } return ""; } // FILE_ANALYSIS_ENGINE_START const IMPORT_ISSUE_LIMIT = 120; function createImportIssue(severity, code, message, lineNumber = null, rawValue = ""){ return { severity: severity || "warning", code: code || "unknown", message: String(message || ""), lineNumber: Number.isFinite(Number(lineNumber)) ? "s" : "")); return visible; } function getReviewCategoryNames(meta){ return uniqueCompactList( meta?.reviewCategories || (state.detectedProperties || []).filter(prop => prop?.needsReview).map(prop => prop.name), Infinity ); } function issueDisplayText(issue, index = 0){ if(!issue) return ""; const label = "Anomalie " + (index + 1); const line = issue.lineNumber ? " - " + raw : ""; return label + " - " + line + code + " - " + safeTrim(issue.message || issue.code || "Anomalie corrig\u00e9e") + suffix; } function getImportIssueNames(meta){ const issues = Array.isArray(meta?.issues) ? meta.issues : (Array.isArray(state.importAnalysis?.issues) ? state.importAnalysis.issues : []); return issues .filter(issue => issue && issue.severity !== "info") .map((issue, index) => issueDisplayText(issue, index)) .filter(Boolean); } function validationItemHtml(titleHtml, details, hasWarning){ const detailHtml = details.length ? '; } function renderImportBadge(meta){ if(!elements.csvActiveBadge) return; ensureImportUiStyles(); setImportControlsLoaded(Boolean(meta), meta); if(!meta){ elements.csvActiveBadge.innerHTML = '

'; return; } const fileName = escapeHtml(meta.fileName || "Fichier"); const fileType = escapeHtml(String(meta.fileType || "").toUpperCase()); const monthCount = Number(meta.monthCount || meta.rows || 0); const processedRows = Number(meta.processedRows || 0); const importedSheets = Array.isArray(meta.importedSheets) ? meta.importedSheets : []; const sheetCount = Number(meta.sheetCount || importedSheets.length || (meta.sheetName ? 1 : 0)); const reviewNames = getReviewCategoryNames(meta); const issueNames = getImportIssueNames(meta); const reviewCount = Number(meta.reviewCount || reviewNames.length || 0); const issueCount = Number(meta.issuesCount || issueNames.length || 0); const alertCount = reviewCount + issueCount; const sourceName = sourceLabel(meta.sourceKind); const source = escapeHtml(sourceName); const sourceShort = sourceName; const displayFileType = fileType; const mode = escapeHtml(importModeLabel(meta.importMode)); const rowsText = processedRows ? processedRows + " ligne" + (processedRows > 1 ? "s" : ""); const sheetList = importedSheets.length ? importedSheets.map(escapeHtml).join(", ") : escapeHtml(meta.sheetName || "Feuille unique"); const alertText = alertCount ? alertCount + " à vérifier" : "Validation OK"; const sourceDetail = [fileName, escapeHtml(sourceShort), displayFileType, sheetText].filter(Boolean).join(" · "); const importDetail = [escapeHtml(monthsText), escapeHtml(rowsText), sheetList, mode].filter(Boolean).join(" · "); const validationSubtitle = alertCount ?
