' + '
' + '
' + '
'; } function readFileText(file){ return readFileArrayBuffer(file).then(buffer => { const decoded = decodeTextBuffer(buffer, file?.name || "fichier"); file._textEncoding = decoded.encoding; file._analysisIssues = decoded.issues || []; return decoded.text; }); } function decodeTextBuffer(buffer, fileName = "fichier"){ const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer || new ArrayBuffer(0)); const issues = []; if(!bytes.length){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "empty_file", "Le fichier est vide.")); return { text: "", encoding: "empty", issues }; } const signatures = [ { bom: [0xEF, 0xBB, 0xBF], encoding: "utf-8" }, { bom: [0xFF, 0xFE], encoding: "utf-16le" }, { bom: [0xFE, 0xFF], encoding: "utf-16be" } ]; const matchedBom = signatures.find(sig => sig.bom.every((value, index) => bytes[index] === value)); const candidates = matchedBom ? [matchedBom.encoding] : ["utf-8", "windows-1252", "iso-8859-1"]; let best = null; for(const encoding of candidates){ try{ const decoder = new TextDecoder(encoding, { fatal: encoding === "utf-8" || encoding.startsWith("utf-16") }); const text = decoder.decode(bytes); const replacementCount = (text.match(/\uFFFD/g) || []).length; const controlCount = (text.match(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F]/g) || []).length; const score = replacementCount * 10 + controlCount * 3; if(!best || score < best.score) best = { text, encoding, score, replacementCount, controlCount }; if(score === 0) break; }catch(err){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "encoding_rejected", "Encodage " + encoding + " refuse avant analyse.", null, err?.message || fileName)); } } if(!best){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "encoding_failed", "Impossible de decoder ce fichier texte proprement.")); return { text: "", encoding: "unknown", issues }; } if(best.replacementCount || best.controlCount){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "encoding_suspicious", "Encodage lisible mais certains caracteres semblent invalides; les donnees exploitables seront conservees.", null, best.encoding)); } return { text: best.text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, ""), encoding: best.encoding, issues }; } function safeTrim(value){ if(value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime())) return value.toISOString(); return String(value ?? "") .replace(/\uFEFF/g, "") .replace(/[\u200B-\u200D\u2060]/g, "") .replace(/\u00A0/g, " ") .replace(/[ \t\f\v]+/g, " ") .replace(/\s*\n+\s*/g, " ") .trim(); } function normalizeValueForCheck(value){ if(value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime())) return value; if(typeof value === "number" || typeof value === "string") return value; if(value === null || value === undefined) return ""; return String(value); } function normalizeNumericText(value){ if(typeof value === "number") return Number.isFinite(value) ? { ok: true, value } : { ok: false, value: NaN }; let text = safeTrim(value); if(!text) return { ok: false, value: NaN }; let negativeByParentheses = false; const paren = text.match(/^\((.*)\)$/); if(paren){ negativeByParentheses = true; text = paren[1]; } text = text .replace(/[‚àí‚Äì‚Äî]/g, "-") .replace(/\b(eur|euro|euros|usd|gbp|chf)\b/gi, "") .replace(/[‚Ç¨$¬£%]/g, "") .replace(/'/g, "") .replace(/\s/g, ""); if(/[a-zA-Z]/.test(text)) return { ok: false, value: NaN }; text = text.replace(/[^\d,.\-+]/g, ""); if(!/[0-9]/.test(text)) return { ok: false, value: NaN }; const sign = text.includes("-") || negativeByParentheses ? "-" : ""; text = text.replace(/[+\-]/g, ""); const commaCount = (text.match(/,/g) || []).length; const dotCount = (text.match(/\./g) || []).length; if(commaCount && dotCount){ const decimal = text.lastIndexOf(",") > text.lastIndexOf(".") ? "," : "."; const thousands = decimal === "," ? "." : ","; text = text.split(thousands).join("").replace(decimal, "."); }else if(commaCount){ const parts = text.split(","); text = commaCount === 1 && parts[1].length <= 2 ? parts[0] + "." + parts[1] : parts.join(""); }else if(dotCount > 1){ text = text.split(".").join(""); }else if(dotCount === 1){ const parts = text.split("."); if(parts[1].length === 3 && parts[0].length <= 3) text = parts.join(""); } const normalized = sign + text; if(!/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(normalized)) return { ok: false, value: NaN }; const num = Number(normalized); return Number.isFinite(num) ? { ok: true, value: num } : { ok: false, value: NaN }; } function isNumericLikeValue(value){ return normalizeNumericText(value).ok; } function toNumberValue(value){ const parsed = normalizeNumericText(value); return parsed.ok ? parsed.value : NaN; } function excelSerialToDate(serial){ const value = Number(serial); if(!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 20000 || value > 80000) return null; const parsed = window.XLSX?.SSF?.parse_date_code ? window.XLSX.SSF.parse_date_code(value) : null; if(parsed && Number.isFinite(parsed.y) && Number.isFinite(parsed.m) && Number.isFinite(parsed.d)){ return makeValidDate(parsed.y, parsed.m, parsed.d); } const utcDays = Math.floor(value - 25569); const utcValue = utcDays * 86400 + Math.floor((value - Math.floor(value)) * 86400); const date = new Date(utcValue * 1000); return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : new Date(date.getFullYear(), date.getMonth(), date.getDate()); } function makeValidDate(year, month, day){ const y = Number(year), m = Number(month), d = Number(day); if(!Number.isInteger(y) || !Number.isInteger(m) || !Number.isInteger(d)) return null; if(y < 1900 || y > 2100 || m < 1 || m > 12 || d < 1 || d > 31) return null; const parsed = new Date(y, m - 1, d); if(parsed.getFullYear() !== y || parsed.getMonth() !== m - 1 || parsed.getDate() !== d) return null; return parsed; } function formatDateIsoLocal(date){ const d = date instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? date : null; if(!d) return ""; const year = String(d.getFullYear()).padStart(4, "0"); const month = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"); const day = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"); return year + "-" + month + "-" + day; } function normalizeYearToken(value){ const y = Number(value); if(!Number.isInteger(y)) return null; if(y >= 0 && y <= 49) return 2000 + y; if(y >= 50 && y <= 99) return 1900 + y; return y; } function monthNumberFromText(value){ const n = normalizePropertyName(value); const months = [ ["janvier", "january", "jan"], ["fevrier", "february", "feb"], ["mars", "march", "mar"], ["avril", "april", "apr"], ["mai", "may"], ["juin", "june", "jun"], ["juillet", "july", "jul"], ["aout", "august", "aug"], ["septembre", "september", "sep"], ["octobre", "october", "oct"], ["novembre", "november", "nov"], ["decembre", "december", "dec"] ]; const idx = months.findIndex(names => names.some(name => n.includes(name))); return idx >= 0 ? idx + 1 : null; } function parseLooseDate(value){ if(value instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(value.getTime())) return new Date(value.getFullYear(), value.getMonth(), value.getDate()); if(typeof value === "number") return excelSerialToDate(value); if(typeof value !== "string") return null; const trimmed = safeTrim(value); if(!trimmed) return null; let match = trimmed.match(/^(\d{4})[-/.](\d{1,2})[-/.](\d{1,2})(?:[ T].*)?$/); if(match) return makeValidDate(Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]), Number(match[3])); match = trimmed.match(/^(\d{1,2})[-/.](\d{1,2})[-/.](\d{2,4})(?:[ T].*)?$/); if(match){ const first = Number(match[1]); const second = Number(match[2]); const year = normalizeYearToken(match[3]); if(year){ if(first > 12) return makeValidDate(year, second, first); if(second > 12) return makeValidDate(year, first, second); return makeValidDate(year, second, first); } } match = trimmed.match(/^(\d{8})$/); if(match) return makeValidDate(Number(match[1].slice(0, 4)), Number(match[1].slice(4, 6)), Number(match[1].slice(6, 8))); const monthNumber = monthNumberFromText(trimmed); const yearMatch = trimmed.match(/\b(20\d{2}|19\d{2})\b/); if(monthNumber && yearMatch){ const dayMatch = trimmed.match(/\b([0-3]?\d)\b/); const day = dayMatch && Number(dayMatch[1]) >= 1 && Number(dayMatch[1]) <= 31 ? Number(dayMatch[1]) : 1; return makeValidDate(Number(yearMatch[1]), monthNumber, day); } if(/[A-Za-z\u00C0-\u017F]/.test(trimmed)) return parseFrenchMonth(trimmed); return null; } function countDelimiterOutsideQuotes(line, delimiter){ let inQuotes = false, count = 0; for(let i = 0; i < line.length; i++){ const ch = line[i]; if(ch === '"'){ if(inQuotes && line[i + 1] === '"'){ i++; continue; } inQuotes = !inQuotes; }else if(ch === delimiter && !inQuotes){ count++; } } return count; } function detectDelimiter(text){ const lines = String(text || "").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").replace(/\r/g, "\n").split("\n").filter(l => safeTrim(l)).slice(0, 24); const candidates = [";", ",", "\t", "|"]; let best = { delimiter: ";", score: -Infinity }; for(const delimiter of candidates){ const counts = lines.map(line => countDelimiterOutsideQuotes(line, delimiter)); const nonZero = counts.filter(v => v > 0).length; if(!nonZero) continue; const mean = counts.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / Math.max(1, counts.length); const variance = counts.reduce((acc, v) => acc + (v - mean) ** 2, 0) / Math.max(1, counts.length); const score = nonZero * 5 + mean * 3 - variance; if(score > best.score) best = { delimiter, score }; } return best.delimiter; } function parseDelimitedRows(text, delimiter){ const issues = []; const source = String(text || "").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").replace(/\r/g, "\n"); const rows = []; let row = [], field = "", inQuotes = false, lineNumber = 1, rowStartLine = 1; function finishField(){ row.push(safeTrim(field)); field = ""; } function finishRow(){ finishField(); const hasValue = row.some(cell => safeTrim(cell) !== ""); const outputRow = row.slice(); outputRow.__lineNumber = rowStartLine; if(hasValue) rows.push(outputRow); else addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("info", "empty_line", "Ligne vide ignoree proprement.", rowStartLine)); row = []; rowStartLine = lineNumber + 1; } for(let i = 0; i < source.length; i++){ const ch = source[i]; if(ch === '"'){ if(inQuotes && source[i + 1] === '"'){ field += '"'; i++; }else if(inQuotes){ inQuotes = false; }else if(field.trim() === ""){ inQuotes = true; }else{ field += ch; addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "quote_in_unquoted_field", "Guillemet inattendu conserve dans une cellule.", lineNumber, field)); } continue; } if(ch === delimiter && !inQuotes){ finishField(); continue; } if(ch === "\n"){ if(inQuotes){ field += "\n"; }else{ finishRow(); } lineNumber++; continue; } field += ch; } if(inQuotes){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "unclosed_quote", "Guillemet non ferme en fin de fichier; la cellule a ete corrigee.", lineNumber, field)); } if(field !== "" || row.length){ finishRow(); } return { rows, issues, lineCount: lineNumber }; } function validateDelimitedStructure(rows, issues){ const widths = (rows || []).map(row => row.filter(cell => safeTrim(cell) !== "").length || row.length).filter(Boolean); if(!widths.length){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "no_tabular_rows", "Aucune ligne tabulaire exploitable detectee.")); return; } const frequency = new Map(); widths.forEach(width => frequency.set(width, (frequency.get(width) || 0) + 1)); const expected = Array.from(frequency.entries()).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || b[0] - a[0])[0][0]; rows.forEach(row => { const width = row.length; if(width && expected && Math.abs(width - expected) >= 2){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "irregular_row_width", "Ligne avec nombre de colonnes atypique; elle sera analysee sans bloquer le fichier.", row.__lineNumber, row.join(" | "))); } }); } function rowsFromDelimitedText(text){ const delimiter = detectDelimiter(text); const parsed = parseDelimitedRows(text, delimiter); validateDelimitedStructure(parsed.rows, parsed.issues); return { rows: parsed.rows, sheetName: delimiter === "\t" ? "TSV" : "CSV", delimiter, issues: parsed.issues, lineCount: parsed.lineCount }; } function cleanRows(rows){ return (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : []) .map((row, index) => { const cleaned = Array.isArray(row) ? row.map(cell => cell instanceof Date ? cell : safeTrim(cell)) : []; cleaned.__lineNumber = row?.__lineNumber ?? index + 1; return cleaned; }) .filter(row => row.some(cell => cell !== undefined && cell !== null && safeTrim(cell) !== "")); } function isBlankSpreadsheetCell(value){ return value === undefined || value === null || (typeof value === "string" && safeTrim(value) === ""); } function rowStats(row){ const values = (Array.isArray(row) ? row : []).filter(cell => cell !== undefined && cell !== null && safeTrim(cell) !== ""); const numeric = values.filter(isNumericLikeValue).length; const dates = values.filter(cell => parseLooseDate(cell)).length; const strings = values.filter(cell => typeof cell === "string" && safeTrim(cell) !== "").length; const unique = new Set(values.map(v => safeTrim(v).toLowerCase())).size; return { count: values.length, numeric, dates, strings, unique }; } function inferFinancialRole(header, samples, context = {}){ const text = [header, context.sheetName, context.fileName].filter(Boolean).join(" "); const normalized = normalizePropertyName(text); if(/id|uuid|hash|reference|ref|code|solde|balance|reste|compte|account|iban|bic/.test(normalized)) return "Ignorer"; if(/salaire|salary|income|revenu|pay|paie|wage|remuneration|bonus|prime/.test(normalized)) return "Salaire"; if(matchesAnyKeyword(text, CATEGORY_DETECTION.savingsKeywords) || /saving|savings|epargne|invest|investment|portfolio|wallet|patrimoine|crypto|placement|livret/.test(normalized)) return "\u00c9pargne"; if(matchesAnyKeyword(text, CATEGORY_DETECTION.expenseKeywords) || /depense|expense|debit|transaction|operation|paiement|payment|withdrawal|retrait|fee|frais|spend|spent|purchase|achat|charge|loyer/.test(normalized)) return "D\u00e9pense"; const numericValues = samples.filter(isNumericLikeValue).map(toNumberValue); const hasManyNumbers = numericValues.length >= Math.max(5, Math.ceil(samples.length * 0.7)); if(hasManyNumbers && /montant|amount|total|valeur|value/.test(normalized)) return "D\u00e9pense"; return null; } function makeUniqueHeaderLabel(raw, index, used, issues, lineNumber){ const base = safeTrim(raw) || "Colonne " + (index + 1); const key = normalizePropertyName(base) || "colonne" + (index + 1); const count = used.get(key) || 0; used.set(key, count + 1); if(count === 0) return base; const renamed = base + " (" + (count + 1) + ")"; addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "duplicate_header", "Colonne dupliquee renommee pour eviter un ecrasement de donnees.", lineNumber, base)); return renamed; } function makeAnalysisReport(table, context = {}){ const issues = mergeImportIssues(table?.issues || [], context?.issues || []); const isExcelSource = normalizePropertyName(table?.sourceKind || "") === "excel"; const rows = cleanRows(table?.rows || []); if(!rows.length){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "empty_table", "Aucune donnee exploitable dans cette feuille.")); return { rows: 0, cols: 0, score: 0, columns: [], sourceKind: table?.sourceKind || "table", sheetName: table?.name || "Feuille 1", sheetSummaries: [], issues, dataRows: [], headers: [], hasHeader: false }; } const scanLimit = Math.min(18, rows.length); let bestHeaderIndex = -1, bestScore = -Infinity; for(let i = 0; i < scanLimit; i++){ const score = scoreHeaderCandidate(rows[i], rows.slice(i + 1, i + 4)); if(score > bestScore){ bestScore = score; bestHeaderIndex = i; } } const hasHeader = bestScore >= 3.5; if(!hasHeader) addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "header_inferred", "En-tete non fiable; des noms de colonnes generiques ont ete crees.")); const headerRow = hasHeader ? rows[bestHeaderIndex] : []; const rawDataRows = hasHeader ? rows.slice(bestHeaderIndex + 1) : rows; const maxCols = Math.max(headerRow.length || 0, ...rawDataRows.map(row => row.length || 0)); const usedHeaders = new Map(); const headers = Array.from({ length: maxCols }, (_, index) => makeUniqueHeaderLabel(hasHeader ? headerRow[index] : "", index, usedHeaders, issues, headerRow.__lineNumber || 1)); const dataRows = rawDataRows.map(row => { const normalized = Array.from({ length: maxCols }, (_, index) => { const value = row[index] ?? ""; return isExcelSource && isBlankSpreadsheetCell(value) ? 0 : value; }); normalized.__lineNumber = row.__lineNumber; return normalized; }); dataRows.forEach(row => { const nonEmpty = row.filter(cell => safeTrim(cell) !== "").length; if(nonEmpty && nonEmpty < Math.max(1, Math.ceil(maxCols * 0.35))){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "sparse_row", "Ligne tres incomplete conservee avec prudence.", row.__lineNumber, row.join(" | "))); } }); const columns = headers.map((header, index) => { const samples = []; for(const row of dataRows){ const value = row[index]; if(value === undefined || value === null || safeTrim(value) === "") continue; samples.push(value); if(samples.length >= 80) break; } const role = inferFinancialRole(header, samples, context); const kind = inferColumnType(header, samples); const numericHits = samples.filter(isNumericLikeValue).length; const dateHits = samples.filter(v => parseLooseDate(v)).length; const nonEmpty = samples.filter(v => safeTrim(v) !== "").length || 1; const confidence = Math.max( role ? 82 : 45, kind.type === "date" ? Math.min(99, 72 + (dateHits / nonEmpty) * 27) : kind.type === "number" ? Math.min(98, 65 + (numericHits / nonEmpty) * 33) : Math.min(96, 54 + (samples.length > 0 ? 18 : 0) + (hasHeader ? 8 : 0)) ); const detailParts = []; if(role) detailParts.push("regle " + role); detailParts.push(kind.label); detailParts.push(samples.length + " echantillon" + (samples.length > 1 ? "s" : "")); if(dateHits) detailParts.push(dateHits + " date" + (dateHits > 1 ? "s" : "")); if(numericHits) detailParts.push(numericHits + " nombre" + (numericHits > 1 ? "s" : "")); return { index, label: header, roleLabel: role || (kind.type === "number" ? "Numerique" : "Texte"), typeLabel: kind.label, kind: kind.type, role, confidence, detail: detailParts.join(" - "), samples }; }); const mappedColumns = columns.filter(column => column.confidence > 60).length; const overallConfidence = Math.max(10, Math.min(99, bestScore + mappedColumns * 2 - issues.filter(issue => issue.severity === "warning").length * 0.25)); return { rows: dataRows.length, cols: maxCols, score: overallConfidence, sourceKind: table?.sourceKind || "table", sheetName: table?.name || "Feuille 1", fileName: context.fileName || "", sheetSummaries: context.sheetSummaries || [], headerIndex: hasHeader ? bestHeaderIndex : null, columns, dataRows, headers, hasHeader, issues }; } function canonicalPropertyName(column){ const header = safeTrim(column?.label || "Colonne " + (Number(column?.index ?? 0) + 1)); const normalized = normalizePropertyName(header); if(column?.kind === "date" || /^(mois|month|date|periode|period|jour|day)$/.test(normalized) || /date|mois|month|periode|period/.test(normalized)) return "Mois"; if(/annee|year/.test(normalized)) return "Ann\u00e9e"; if(column?.role === "Salaire" || /salaire|salary|income|revenu|remuneration|wage|paie/.test(normalized)) return "Salaire"; return header || "Colonne " + (Number(column?.index ?? 0) + 1); } function columnAutoRule(column){ const header = safeTrim(column?.label || ""); const normalized = normalizePropertyName(header); if(column?.role === "Salaire" || /salaire|salary|income|revenu|remuneration|wage|paie/.test(normalized)) return "Salaire"; if(column?.role === "\u00c9pargne" || /epargne|saving|savings|invest|investment|portfolio|wallet|placement|livret|pea|bourse|bourso/.test(normalized)) return "\u00c9pargne"; if(column?.role === "D\u00e9pense" || /depense|charge|facture|loyer|expense|expenses|montant|amount|debit|transaction|operation|paiement|payment|withdrawal|retrait|frais|fee|spend|spent|achat|purchase/.test(normalized)) return "D\u00e9pense"; if(column?.role === "Ignorer") return "Ignorer"; return classifyFinancialProperty(header); } function buildPropertyValue(column, value, rowIndex, issues = [], lineNumber = null){ if(value === undefined || value === null || safeTrim(value) === "") return null; const dateValue = parseLooseDate(value); if(column?.kind === "date"){ if(dateValue) return { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(dateValue) } }; const fallback = safeTrim(value); addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "invalid_date_value", "Date invalide convertie en libelle texte.", lineNumber, value)); return { type: "title", title: [{ plain_text: fallback || "Mois " + (rowIndex + 1) }] }; } if(column?.kind === "number"){ const parsed = toNumberValue(value); if(!Number.isFinite(parsed)){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "invalid_number_value", "Nombre invalide ignore sans bloquer la ligne.", lineNumber, value)); return null; } const autoRule = columnAutoRule(column); const confidence = autoRule && autoRule !== "Ignorer" ? 0.78 : 0.25; return numberProperty(parsed, autoRule, confidence, !autoRule || autoRule === "Ignorer"); } const text = safeTrim(value); return text ? { type: "rich_text", rich_text: [{ plain_text: text }] } : null; } function normalizePropertyForRow(column, value, rowIndex, issues = [], lineNumber = null){ const canonical = canonicalPropertyName(column); if(canonical === "Mois"){ const dateValue = parseLooseDate(value); if(dateValue) return { name: "Mois", value: { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(dateValue) } } }; const text = safeTrim(value); addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "invalid_month_value", "Mois/date non reconnu; libelle texte utilise.", lineNumber, value)); return { name: "Mois", value: { type: "title", title: [{ plain_text: text || "Mois " + (rowIndex + 1) }] } }; } if(canonical === "Ann\u00e9e"){ const num = toNumberValue(value); return { name: "Ann\u00e9e", value: Number.isFinite(num) ? { type: "number", number: num } : { type: "rich_text", rich_text: [{ plain_text: safeTrim(value) }] } }; } const prop = buildPropertyValue(column, value, rowIndex, issues, lineNumber); return prop ? { name: canonical, value: prop } : null; } function assignMappedProperty(properties, name, value, issues, lineNumber){ if(!name || !value) return false; let finalName = safeTrim(name); const base = finalName; let suffix = 2; while(Object.keys(properties).some(existing => normalizePropertyName(existing) === normalizePropertyName(finalName))){ finalName = base + " " + suffix; suffix++; } if(finalName !== base){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "mapping_collision", "Deux colonnes pointaient vers le meme champ; un nom unique a ete cree pour eviter l'ecrasement.", lineNumber, base)); } properties[finalName] = value; return true; } function isImportableSheetAnalysis(item, totalSheets){ const report = item?.report; if(!report || !(report.dataRows || []).length) return false; const sheetName = String(report.sheetName || item.name || ""); const normalizedSheet = normalizePropertyName(sheetName); const yearNamed = /\b(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})\b/.test(sheetName); const noisySheet = /dashboard|tableaudebord|synthese|synthesis|resume|summary|param|config|notes|modele|template/.test(normalizedSheet); if(noisySheet && !yearNamed && totalSheets > 1) return false; if(report.importMode === "matrix") return true; if(totalSheets <= 1) return true; if(yearNamed) return true; if(report.importMode === "ledger") return item.selectionScore >= 240; if(report.importMode === "summary") return item.selectionScore >= 210; return false; } function attachWorkbookSummary(report, sheetSummaries, importedReports){ if(!report) return report; report.sheetSummaries = sheetSummaries; report.importedSheetNames = (importedReports || []).map(item => item.sheetName || item.name || "Feuille"); report.importedSheetCount = report.importedSheetNames.length; return report; } function analyzeWorkbookSheets(tables, context){ const analyzedSheets = (tables || []).map(table => { const report = makeAnalysisReport(table, context); report.fileName = context?.fileName || ""; report.sheetName = table.name || report.sheetName || "Feuille 1"; report.importMode = detectImportMode(report); const modePriority = report.importMode === "matrix" ? 300 : report.importMode === "ledger" ? 220 : 120; const detailBonus = /\b(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})\b/.test(String(report.sheetName || "")) ? 35 : 0; const synthesisPenalty = /synthese|synthesis|dashboard|resume|summary/.test(normalizePropertyName(report.sheetName || "")) ? -45 : 0; const selectionScore = modePriority + detailBonus + synthesisPenalty + Math.min(report.rows || 0, 80) + Math.min(report.cols || 0, 30) + (report.score || 0) / 10; return { name: table.name || "Feuille 1", rows: report.rows, cols: report.cols, score: report.score, selectionScore, mode: report.importMode, sourceKind: report.sourceKind, issues: report.issues || [], report }; }).sort((a, b) => b.selectionScore - a.selectionScore || b.rows - a.rows || b.cols - a.cols || b.score - a.score); const sheetSummaries = analyzedSheets.map(({ report, ...summary }) => summary); const importableReports = analyzedSheets .filter(item => isImportableSheetAnalysis(item, analyzedSheets.length)) .map(item => attachWorkbookSummary(item.report, sheetSummaries, analyzedSheets.filter(other => isImportableSheetAnalysis(other, analyzedSheets.length)).map(other => other.report))); const chosen = attachWorkbookSummary(importableReports[0] || analyzedSheets[0]?.report || null, sheetSummaries, importableReports); return { chosen, sheetSummaries, importableReports }; } function tableFromXlsxWorkbook(workbook){ const sheets = []; (workbook?.SheetNames || []).forEach(name => { const sheet = workbook.Sheets?.[name]; if(!sheet) return; const rows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet, { header: 1, raw: true, defval: "", blankrows: false }); rows.forEach((row, index) => { if(Array.isArray(row)) row.__lineNumber = index + 1; }); sheets.push({ name, rows, sourceKind: "Excel", issues: [] }); }); return sheets; } function tableFromTextFile(fileName, text, inheritedIssues = []){ const extension = (fileName.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase(); const trimmed = String(text || "").trim(); const issues = mergeImportIssues(inheritedIssues); if(!trimmed){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "empty_text_file", "Le fichier texte ne contient aucune donnee.")); return [{ name: "Texte", rows: [], sourceKind: "Texte", issues }]; } if(extension === "json" || trimmed.startsWith("[") || trimmed.startsWith("{")){ try{ const parsed = JSON.parse(trimmed); return [{ name: "JSON", rows: jsonToRows(parsed), sourceKind: "JSON", issues }]; }catch(err){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "json_parse_failed", "JSON invalide; tentative de lecture comme texte delimite.", null, err?.message || "")); } } if(extension === "xml" || trimmed.startsWith(")/i.test(trimmed)){ const rows = xmlToRows(trimmed); if(rows.length) return [{ name: "XML", rows, sourceKind: "XML", issues }]; const htmlRows = htmlTableToRows(trimmed); if(htmlRows.length) return [{ name: "HTML", rows: htmlRows, sourceKind: "HTML", issues }]; if(extension === "xml" || extension === "html" || extension === "htm") addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "markup_without_table", "Balises detectees mais aucun tableau exploitable; lecture CSV tentee.")); } const parsed = rowsFromDelimitedText(trimmed); return [{ name: parsed.sheetName || "CSV", rows: parsed.rows, sourceKind: extension === "tsv" ? "TSV" : "CSV", delimiter: parsed.delimiter, issues: mergeImportIssues(issues, parsed.issues || []) }]; } function tableFromFile(file, workbook){ if(workbook) return tableFromXlsxWorkbook(workbook); return tableFromTextFile(file.name, file._text || "", file._analysisIssues || []); } function extractYearFromText(...values){ for(const value of values){ const match = safeTrim(value).match(/\b(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})\b/); if(match) return Number(match[1]); } return null; } function matrixContextYear(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const rowText = (analysis?.dataRows || []).slice(0, 12).map(row => (Array.isArray(row) ? row.join(" ") : "")).join(" "); return extractYearFromText(sheetName, fileName, rowText) || new Date().getFullYear(); } function monthDateFromLabel(label, year){ const text = safeTrim(label); if(!text || /total|somme|bilan|reste|solde|balance/i.test(text)) return null; const explicit = parseLooseDate(text); if(explicit && /\b(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})\b/.test(text)) return monthKeyFromDate(explicit); const monthNumber = monthNumberFromText(text); if(!monthNumber) return null; return makeValidDate(Number(year) || new Date().getFullYear(), monthNumber, 1); } function getMatrixDescriptorColumn(analysis){ const columns = analysis?.columns || []; return columns.find(column => /categorie|category|poste|libelle|label|description|nom|name/.test(normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(column)))) || pickBestColumn(columns, scoreTextColumn); } function getMatrixMonthColumns(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const year = matrixContextYear(analysis, fileName, sheetName); const labelColumn = getMatrixDescriptorColumn(analysis); const monthColumns = (analysis?.columns || []) .filter(column => column.index !== labelColumn?.index) .map(column => ({ column, date: monthDateFromLabel(getColumnText(column), year) })) .filter(item => item.date); return { labelColumn, monthColumns, year }; } function isMatrixAnalysis(analysis, fileName = "", sheetName = ""){ const { labelColumn, monthColumns } = getMatrixMonthColumns(analysis, fileName, sheetName); if(!labelColumn || monthColumns.length < 1) return false; const labels = (analysis?.dataRows || []).map(row => normalizePropertyName(row?.[labelColumn.index])).filter(Boolean); const hasFinancialRows = labels.some(label => /salaire|revenu|depense|charge|loyer|epargne|livret|bourse|pea|bilan/.test(label)); return hasFinancialRows; } function pickBestColumn(columns, scorer, excludedIndexes = new Set()){ return (columns || []) .filter(column => !excludedIndexes.has(column.index)) .map(column => ({ column, score: Number(scorer(column)) || 0 })) .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.column.index - b.column.index)[0]?.column || null; } function detectImportMode(analysis){ const columns = analysis?.columns || []; const rows = analysis?.dataRows || []; if(isMatrixAnalysis(analysis, analysis?.fileName || "", analysis?.sheetName || "")) return "matrix"; const dateCol = pickBestColumn(columns, scoreDateColumn); const amountLikeCols = columns.filter(c => scoreAmountColumn(c) >= 4.5); const textLikeCols = columns.filter(c => scoreTextColumn(c) >= 4); const hasSummaryMarkers = columns.some(c => /salaire|salary|revenu|income|epargne|budget|objectif|objective/.test(normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(c)))); const hasTransactionMarkers = columns.some(c => /transaction|operation|libell|label|merchant|categorie|category|description|beneficiaire|tiers/.test(normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(c)))); if(dateCol && amountLikeCols.length >= 1 && rows.length >= 2 && (hasTransactionMarkers || textLikeCols.length >= 1)) return "ledger"; if(hasSummaryMarkers || amountLikeCols.length >= 3) return "summary"; if(dateCol && amountLikeCols.length === 1 && rows.length >= 6) return "ledger"; return "summary"; } function detectMatrixSection(label, currentSection){ const normalized = normalizePropertyName(label); if(!normalized) return currentSection; if(/revenu|income|recette/.test(normalized)) return "income"; if(/depense|charge|expense|cout|co[u√ª]t/.test(normalized)) return "expense"; if(/epargne|saving|savings|placement|patrimoine/.test(normalized)) return "saving"; if(/bilan|reste|disponible|solde|balance/.test(normalized)) return "balance"; return currentSection; } function shouldSkipMatrixRow(label){ const normalized = normalizePropertyName(label); if(!normalized) return true; if(/soustotal|subtotal|totalrevenu|totaldepense|totalepargne|total|bilanmensuel|restedisponible|indicateur|navigue|info/.test(normalized)) return true; return /^(revenus|epargne|bilan)$/.test(normalized); } function cleanMatrixLabel(label){ return safeTrim(label).replace(/^[^\p{L}\p{N}]+/u, "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || "Autres"; } function matrixPropertyMeta(label, section){ const clean = cleanMatrixLabel(label); const normalized = normalizePropertyName(clean); if(/salaire|salary|paie|pay/.test(normalized)) return { name: "Salaire", rule: "Salaire", confidence: 0.99 }; if(/reste|solde|balance|disponible/.test(normalized)) return { name: clean, rule: "Ignorer", confidence: 0.35, needsReview: true }; if(/epargne|livret|pea|bourse|placement|saving|savings|invest|patrimoine/.test(normalized)) return { name: clean, rule: "\u00c9pargne", confidence: 0.9 }; if(/compte|revolut|bourso|boursobank|bank|banque/.test(normalized)) return { name: clean, rule: "\u00c9pargne", confidence: 0.48, needsReview: true }; if(/depense|charge|loyer|carte|vacance|frais|fee|expense|achat|course|abonnement|assurance|impot|taxe/.test(normalized)) return { name: clean, rule: "D\u00e9pense", confidence: 0.9 }; if(section === "expense") return { name: clean, rule: "D\u00e9pense", confidence: 0.72 }; if(section === "saving") return { name: clean, rule: "\u00c9pargne", confidence: 0.72 }; if(section === "income") return null; return { name: clean, rule: "Ignorer", confidence: 0.15, needsReview: true }; } function numberProperty(value, rule, confidence = 0.8, needsReview = false){ return { type: "number", number: Math.abs(Number(value) || 0), _autoRule: rule || "Ignorer", _autoConfidence: confidence, _needsReview: Boolean(needsReview || !rule || rule === "Ignorer" || confidence < 0.55) }; } function inferPropertyRule(propName, prop){ if(prop && typeof prop === "object" && prop._autoRule) return prop._autoRule; return classifyFinancialProperty(propName); } function inferPropertyConfidence(propName, prop){ if(prop && typeof prop === "object" && Number.isFinite(Number(prop._autoConfidence))) return Number(prop._autoConfidence); const ex = classifyFinancialPropertyEx(propName); if(!ex.rule || ex.rule === "Ignorer") return 0.2; return ex.matched ? 0.85 : 0.2; } function propertyNeedsReview(propName, prop){ if(prop && typeof prop === "object" && prop._needsReview) return true; if(isMetaPropertyName(propName)) return false; const auto = inferPropertyRule(propName, prop); const confidence = inferPropertyConfidence(propName, prop); return !auto || auto === "Ignorer" || confidence < 0.55; } function buildMatrixPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const issues = analysis.issues || []; const { labelColumn, monthColumns } = getMatrixMonthColumns(analysis, fileName, sheetName); if(!labelColumn || !monthColumns.length){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "matrix_mapping_failed", "Tableau mois-en-colonnes non reconnu; bascule en mode synthese.")); return buildSummaryPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName); } const pages = []; monthColumns.forEach(({ column, date }, monthIndex) => { const properties = { Mois: { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(date) } } }; let section = null; let mapped = 0; (analysis?.dataRows || []).forEach(row => { const label = row?.[labelColumn.index]; const cleanLabel = cleanMatrixLabel(label); const rowHasNumericValue = monthColumns.some(({ column }) => Number.isFinite(toNumberValue(row?.[column.index])) && toNumberValue(row?.[column.index]) !== 0); if(!rowHasNumericValue) section = detectMatrixSection(cleanLabel, section); if(shouldSkipMatrixRow(cleanLabel)) return; const meta = matrixPropertyMeta(cleanLabel, section); if(!meta) return; const value = toNumberValue(row?.[column.index]); if(!Number.isFinite(value)) return; assignMappedProperty(properties, meta.name, numberProperty(value, meta.rule, meta.confidence, meta.needsReview), issues, row?.__lineNumber); mapped++; }); if(mapped > 0){ pages.push({ id: "import-" + normalizePropertyName(fileName || "file") + "-" + normalizePropertyName(sheetName || "sheet") + "-matrix-" + (monthIndex + 1), properties }); }else{ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("info", "empty_matrix_month", "Colonne mois ignoree car aucune valeur financiere exploitable.", null, getColumnText(column))); } }); if(!pages.length) addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "matrix_no_pages", "Aucun mois exploitable dans le tableau matriciel.")); return pages; } function buildSummaryPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const pages = []; const issues = analysis.issues || []; (analysis?.dataRows || []).forEach((row, rowIndex) => { try{ const properties = {}; const normalizedRow = Array.isArray(row) ? row : []; let hasAny = false; (analysis.columns || []).forEach(column => { const cell = normalizedRow[column.index]; if(cell === undefined || cell === null || safeTrim(cell) === "") return; const prop = normalizePropertyForRow(column, cell, rowIndex, issues, normalizedRow.__lineNumber); if(!prop) return; if(assignMappedProperty(properties, prop.name, prop.value, issues, normalizedRow.__lineNumber)) hasAny = true; }); if(!hasAny){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "row_without_mappable_data", "Ligne ignoree: aucune donnee exploitable apres validation.", normalizedRow.__lineNumber, normalizedRow.join(" | "))); return; } if(!Object.keys(properties).some(name => normalizePropertyName(name) === normalizePropertyName("Mois"))){ const firstDate = (analysis.columns || []).map(col => normalizedRow[col.index]).find(v => parseLooseDate(v)); const parsed = parseLooseDate(firstDate); if(parsed) properties.Mois = { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(parsed) } }; else{ const firstText = getRowFirstText(normalizedRow); properties.Mois = { type: "title", title: [{ plain_text: firstText || "Mois " + (rowIndex + 1) }] }; addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "missing_month", "Aucune date fiable; libelle de mois genere.", normalizedRow.__lineNumber, firstText)); } } pages.push({ id: "import-" + normalizePropertyName(fileName || "file") + "-" + normalizePropertyName(sheetName || "sheet") + "-" + (rowIndex + 1), properties }); }catch(err){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "row_parse_exception", "Ligne ignoree apres erreur locale: " + (err?.message || err), row?.__lineNumber, Array.isArray(row) ? row.join(" | ") : "")); } }); return pages; } function coerceLedgerAmount(row, amountColumns, fallbackColumns, dateCol){ const candidates = [...amountColumns, ...fallbackColumns].filter((column, index, all) => column && column.index !== dateCol?.index && all.findIndex(other => other.index === column.index) === index); for(const column of candidates){ const raw = row[column.index]; const parsed = toNumberValue(raw); if(!Number.isFinite(parsed) || parsed === 0) continue; const label = normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(column)); let value = parsed; if(/debit|depense|retrait|withdrawal|payment|paiement/.test(label) && parsed > 0) value = -Math.abs(parsed); if(/credit|income|revenu|salaire|deposit|versement/.test(label) && parsed > 0) value = Math.abs(parsed); return { value, raw, column }; } return { value: NaN, raw: "", column: null }; } function buildLedgerPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const columns = analysis?.columns || []; const rows = analysis?.dataRows || []; const issues = analysis.issues || []; if(!columns.length || !rows.length) return []; const dateCol = pickBestColumn(columns, scoreDateColumn); const amountColumns = columns.filter(c => c.index !== dateCol?.index && scoreAmountColumn(c) >= 4.5).sort((a, b) => scoreAmountColumn(b) - scoreAmountColumn(a)); const amountCol = amountColumns[0] || pickBestColumn(columns, scoreAmountColumn, new Set([dateCol?.index])); const excluded = new Set([dateCol?.index, amountCol?.index].filter(Number.isFinite)); const categoryCol = pickBestColumn(columns, scoreTextColumn, excluded); const labelCol = (columns || []).find(c => !excluded.has(c.index) && /libell|label|description|merchant|beneficiaire|transaction|operation/.test(normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(c)))) || categoryCol; if(!dateCol || !amountCol || dateCol.index === amountCol.index){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "ledger_mapping_failed", "Colonnes date/montant non distinctes; bascule en mode synthese.")); return buildSummaryPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName); } const buckets = new Map(); const fallbackAmountColumns = columns.filter(c => c !== dateCol && c !== categoryCol && c !== labelCol); function ensureBucket(key, date){ if(!buckets.has(key)){ buckets.set(key, { date, salary: 0, expenses: 0, savings: 0, expenseDetails: new Map(), savingsDetails: new Map(), incomeDetails: new Map(), notes: [], rowCount: 0 }); } return buckets.get(key); } rows.forEach((row) => { const normalizedRow = Array.isArray(row) ? row : []; try{ const parsedDate = parseLooseDate(normalizedRow[dateCol.index]); const bucketDate = monthKeyFromDate(parsedDate); if(!bucketDate){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "ledger_row_bad_date", "Transaction ignoree: date invalide.", normalizedRow.__lineNumber, normalizedRow[dateCol.index])); return; } const amountChoice = coerceLedgerAmount(normalizedRow, amountColumns.length ? amountColumns : [amountCol], fallbackAmountColumns, dateCol); let amount = amountChoice.value; if(!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount === 0){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "ledger_row_bad_amount", "Transaction ignoree: montant invalide.", normalizedRow.__lineNumber, amountChoice.raw)); return; } const key = formatDateIsoLocal(bucketDate); const bucket = ensureBucket(key, bucketDate); const categoryText = getRowFirstText(normalizedRow, [categoryCol?.index, labelCol?.index]) || "Autres"; const rowText = [categoryText, getColumnText(categoryCol), getColumnText(labelCol), getColumnText(amountChoice.column || amountCol)].filter(Boolean).join(" "); const rowKind = inferFinancialRole(rowText, [amountChoice.raw], { fileName, sheetName }) || ""; const amountAbs = Math.abs(amount); const categoryNorm = normalizePropertyName(categoryText); const isSavings = /epargne|saving|savings|invest|investment|livret|wallet|crypto|placement|transfer|virement/.test(categoryNorm) || rowKind === "\u00c9pargne"; const isIncome = /salaire|salary|income|revenu|pay|paie|remuneration|bonus|refund|credit|deposit|versement/.test(categoryNorm) || rowKind === "Salaire" || amount > 0 && /credit|income|revenu|salaire|deposit|versement/.test(normalizePropertyName(getColumnText(amountChoice.column))); const isExpense = !isSavings && (amount < 0 || /depense|expense|achat|purchase|fee|frais|retrait|withdrawal|payment|debit|bill|facture|tax|impot|loyer|rent|restaurant|food|course|transport|travel|voyage|subscription|abonnement/.test(categoryNorm) || rowKind === "D\u00e9pense"); const normalizedName = safeTrim(categoryText) || "Autres"; if(isSavings){ bucket.savings += amountAbs; bucket.savingsDetails.set(normalizedName, (bucket.savingsDetails.get(normalizedName) || 0) + amountAbs); }else if(isIncome){ bucket.salary += amountAbs; bucket.incomeDetails.set(normalizedName, (bucket.incomeDetails.get(normalizedName) || 0) + amountAbs); }else if(isExpense || amountAbs > 0){ bucket.expenses += amountAbs; bucket.expenseDetails.set(normalizedName, (bucket.expenseDetails.get(normalizedName) || 0) + amountAbs); } bucket.rowCount += 1; if(normalizedName && bucket.notes.length < 8) bucket.notes.push(normalizedName + ": " + formatEuro(amountAbs)); }catch(err){ addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("warning", "ledger_row_exception", "Transaction ignoree apres erreur locale: " + (err?.message || err), normalizedRow.__lineNumber, normalizedRow.join(" | "))); } }); return Array.from(buckets.entries()).sort((a, b) => a[0].localeCompare(b[0])).map(([key, bucket], idx) => { const properties = { Mois: { type: "date", date: { start: key } } }; if(bucket.salary > 0) properties.Salaire = { type: "number", number: bucket.salary }; if(bucket.expenses > 0) properties["D\u00e9penses globales"] = { type: "number", number: bucket.expenses }; if(bucket.savings > 0) properties["\u00c9pargne globale"] = { type: "number", number: bucket.savings }; Array.from(bucket.expenseDetails.entries()).forEach(([name, value]) => assignMappedProperty(properties, safeTrim(name) || "Autres", numberProperty(value, "D\u00e9pense", 0.88), issues, null)); Array.from(bucket.savingsDetails.entries()).forEach(([name, value]) => assignMappedProperty(properties, safeTrim(name) || "Autres", numberProperty(value, "\u00c9pargne", 0.88), issues, null)); Array.from(bucket.incomeDetails.entries()).forEach(([name, value]) => { const safeName = safeTrim(name) || "Revenu"; if(normalizePropertyName(safeName) !== normalizePropertyName("Salaire")) assignMappedProperty(properties, safeName, numberProperty(value, "Salaire", 0.82), issues, null); }); const notes = bucket.notes.slice(0, 4).join(" - "); if(notes) properties["Grosses d\u00e9penses"] = { type: "rich_text", rich_text: [{ plain_text: notes }] }; return { id: "import-" + normalizePropertyName(fileName || "file") + "-" + normalizePropertyName(sheetName || "sheet") + "-" + (idx + 1), properties }; }); } function buildPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName){ const issues = analysis.issues || []; const mode = detectImportMode(analysis); const pages = mode === "matrix" ? buildMatrixPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName) : mode === "ledger" ? buildLedgerPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName) : buildSummaryPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName); const validPages = pages.filter(page => page && page.properties && Object.keys(page.properties).length > 0); // Attache l'ann√©e du nom de feuille √† toutes les pages const sheetYear = extractYearFromText(sheetName) || extractYearFromText(fileName); if(sheetYear){ validPages.forEach(page => { // Ajoute la propri√©t√© Ann√©e if(!page.properties["Ann\u00e9e"] && !page.properties["Annee"] && !page.properties["Year"]){ page.properties["Ann\u00e9e"] = { type: "rich_text", rich_text: [{ type: "text", text: String(sheetYear), plain_text: String(sheetYear) }] }; } // Corrige l'ann√©e du Mois si elle est incorrecte const moisProp = page.properties["Mois"]; if(moisProp && moisProp.type === "date" && moisProp.date && moisProp.date.start){ var d = parseIsoDateLocal(moisProp.date.start); if(d && d.getFullYear() !== sheetYear){ d.setFullYear(sheetYear); moisProp.date.start = formatDateIsoLocal(d); } } }); } if(!validPages.length) addImportIssue(issues, createImportIssue("error", "no_pages_after_mapping", "Aucune ligne n'a pu etre mappee apres validation.")); analysis.importMode = mode; return validPages; } function buildPagesFromWorkbookBundle(bundle, fileName){ const reports = (bundle?.importableReports || []).length ? bundle.importableReports : (bundle?.chosen ? [bundle.chosen] : []); const pages = []; const analyses = []; const issues = []; reports.forEach(analysis => { if(!analysis) return; const sheetName = analysis.sheetName || "Feuille"; const sheetPages = buildPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fileName, sheetName); if(sheetPages.length){ pages.push(...sheetPages); analyses.push(analysis); } (analysis.issues || []).forEach(issue => addImportIssue(issues, issue)); }); const importedSheets = analyses.map(analysis => analysis.sheetName || "Feuille"); const processedRows = analyses.reduce((total, analysis) => total + Number((analysis.dataRows || []).length || analysis.rows || 0), 0); const maxCols = analyses.reduce((max, analysis) => Math.max(max, Number(analysis.cols || 0)), 0); const modes = Array.from(new Set(analyses.map(analysis => analysis.importMode).filter(Boolean))); return { pages, analyses, issues, importedSheets, processedRows, maxCols, importMode: modes.length > 1 ? "mixed" : (modes[0] || bundle?.chosen?.importMode || "summary") }; } function buildCategoryModel(pages){ const propRules = getPropertyRules(); const avail = getAvailableRules(); const detected = new Map(); pages.forEach(page => { Object.entries(page.properties || {}).forEach(([pname, prop]) => { if(!isNumberLikeProperty(prop)) return; if(isMetaPropertyName(pname)) return; const rkey = normalizePropertyName(pname); const auto = inferPropertyRule(pname, prop) || "Ignorer"; const confidence = inferPropertyConfidence(pname, prop); const needsReview = propertyNeedsReview(pname, prop); const saved = migrateRuleValue(propRules[rkey] || "auto"); const eff = saved === "auto" ? auto : saved; const color = getRuleColor(eff || auto || "Ignorer"); if(!detected.has(pname)){ detected.set(pname, { id: rkey, propertyName: pname, name: safeTrim(pname), automaticRule: needsReview ? "√Ä confirmer" : auto, detectedRule: auto, savedRule: saved, rule: eff || "Ignorer", color, confidence, needsReview }); } }); }); return { detectedProperties: Array.from(detected.values()).sort(sortDetectedProperties), availableRules: avail }; } function sortDetectedProperties(a, b){ if(Boolean(a.needsReview) !== Boolean(b.needsReview)) return a.needsReview ? -1 : 1; const order = { "D\u00e9pense": 0, "√âpargne": 1, "Salaire": 2, "Bourse": 3, "Ignorer": 4, "√Ä confirmer": -1 }; const ak = a.detectedRule || a.rule || "Ignorer"; const bk = b.detectedRule || b.rule || "Ignorer"; const ao = order[ak] ?? 99; const bo = order[bk] ?? 99; return ao !== bo ? ao - bo : a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "fr"); } function renderCategorySettings(){ elements.categorySettings.hidden = state.detectedProperties.length === 0; elements.categorySettingsList.innerHTML = ""; const title = elements.categorySettings?.querySelector(".category-settings-title h3"); if(title) title.textContent = "Cat\u00e9gories d\u00e9tect\u00e9es (" + state.detectedProperties.length + ")"; const avail = getAvailableRules(); state.detectedProperties.forEach(prop => { const row = document.createElement("div"); row.className = "category-row" + (prop.needsReview ? " category-row-review" : ""); const nameDiv = document.createElement("div"); nameDiv.className = "category-name"; const swatch = document.createElement("span"); swatch.className = "category-color"; swatch.style.background = getRuleColor(prop.rule || prop.detectedRule || prop.automaticRule); const label = document.createElement("span"); label.textContent = prop.name; if(prop.needsReview) label.title = "Cat\u00e9gorie \u00e0 confirmer"; const select = document.createElement("select"); select.dataset.categoryRule = prop.id; select.className = "native-select-hidden"; const autoOpt = document.createElement("option"); autoOpt.value = "auto"; autoOpt.textContent = prop.needsReview ? "\u00c0 confirmer" : "Auto (" + (prop.automaticRule || "Ignorer") + ")"; select.appendChild(autoOpt); avail.forEach(rule => { const opt = document.createElement("option"); opt.value = rule; opt.textContent = rule; select.appendChild(opt); }); select.value = prop.savedRule || "auto"; nameDiv.append(swatch, label); row.append(nameDiv, select); elements.categorySettingsList.appendChild(row); createCustomDropdown(select); }); } function parseFinancePage(page, index, categoryModel){ const props = page.properties || {}; const monthProp = findProperty(props, PROPERTY_NAMES.month); const yearProp = findProperty(props, PROPERTY_NAMES.year); const salaryProp = findProperty(props, PROPERTY_NAMES.salary); const bigExpProp = findProperty(props, PROPERTY_NAMES.bigExpenses); const monthLabel = getPropertyText(monthProp) || "Mois " + (index + 1); const yearLabel = getPropertyText(yearProp); const monthDate = getPropertyDate(monthProp) || parseFrenchMonth([monthLabel, yearLabel].filter(Boolean).join(" ")) || parseFrenchMonth(monthLabel); const propRules = getPropertyRules(); let salaryTotal = 0; let hasIncome = false; let hasExpenses = false; let hasSavings = false; const ruleTotals = new Map(); const ruleDetails = new Map(); Object.entries(props).forEach(([pname, prop]) => { if(!isNumberLikeProperty(prop)) return; if(isMetaPropertyName(pname)) return; const val = getPropertyNumber(prop); const rkey = normalizePropertyName(pname); const saved = migrateRuleValue(propRules[rkey] || "auto"); const auto = inferPropertyRule(pname, prop); const eff = saved === "auto" ? auto : saved; if(eff === "Salaire"){ salaryTotal += val; hasIncome = true; return; } if(!eff || eff === "Ignorer") return; if(eff === "D\u00e9pense") hasExpenses = true; if(eff === "\u00c9pargne") hasSavings = true; ruleTotals.set(eff, (ruleTotals.get(eff) || 0) + val); if(!ruleDetails.has(eff)) ruleDetails.set(eff, []); ruleDetails.get(eff).push({ name: pname, value: val }); }); const avail = (categoryModel?.availableRules || getAvailableRules()).filter(rule => rule !== "Ignorer" && rule !== "Salaire"); const ruleBreakdown = avail .map(rule => ({ name: rule, color: getRuleColor(rule), value: ruleTotals.get(rule) || 0 })) .filter(item => item.value !== 0 || item.name === "D\u00e9pense" || item.name === "\u00c9pargne"); const savingsTotal = ruleTotals.get("\u00c9pargne") || 0; const expensesTotal = ruleTotals.get("D\u00e9pense") || 0; return { id: page.id, label: formatMonthLabel(monthLabel, monthDate), originalLabel: monthLabel, date: monthDate, index, salary: salaryTotal || getPropertyNumber(salaryProp), hasIncome, hasExpenses, hasSavings, expenses: expensesTotal, ruleBreakdown, ruleDetails: Object.fromEntries(ruleDetails.entries()), bigExpenses: getPropertyText(bigExpProp) || "-", savingsTotal }; } function normalizeFinanceDataset(pages){ const cleanPages = (pages || []).filter(page => page && page.properties && Object.keys(page.properties).length > 0); const categoryModel = buildCategoryModel(cleanPages); const rawMonths = cleanPages .map((page, index) => parseFinancePage(page, index, categoryModel)) .filter(month => month && (month.date || month.label)); // D√©doublonne les mois : fusionne les pages qui r√©solvent vers le m√™me mois (YYYY-MM). // Corrige les doublons apparus quand un fichier matrix contient plusieurs fois le m√™me mois. const months = dedupMonths(rawMonths).sort(sortMonths); return { pages: cleanPages, categoryModel, months }; } function canonicalMonthKey(month){ if(month.date instanceof Date && !isNaN(month.date.getTime())){ return month.date.getFullYear() + "-" + String(month.date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"); } return normalizePropertyName(month.label || month.originalLabel || ""); } function mergeMonths(target, source){ target.salary = (Number(target.salary) || 0) + (Number(source.salary) || 0); target.savingsTotal = (Number(target.savingsTotal) || 0) + (Number(source.savingsTotal) || 0); target.expenses = (Number(target.expenses) || 0) + (Number(source.expenses) || 0); const acc = new Map(); [...(target.ruleBreakdown || []), ...(source.ruleBreakdown || [])].forEach(item => { if(!item || !item.name) return; if(!acc.has(item.name)) acc.set(item.name, Object.assign({}, item)); else acc.get(item.name).value = (Number(acc.get(item.name).value) || 0) + (Number(item.value) || 0); }); target.ruleBreakdown = Array.from(acc.values()).filter(i => i.value !== 0 || i.name === "D\u00e9pense" || i.name === "\u00c9pargne"); const details = Object.assign({}, target.ruleDetails || {}); Object.entries(source.ruleDetails || {}).forEach(([key, list]) => { details[key] = (details[key] || []).concat(list || []); }); target.ruleDetails = details; return target; } function dedupMonths(months){ const acc = new Map(); const order = []; (months || []).forEach(month => { const key = canonicalMonthKey(month); if(!acc.has(key)){ acc.set(key, Object.assign({}, month)); order.push(key); } else mergeMonths(acc.get(key), month); }); return order.map(key => acc.get(key)); } function applyFinancePagesToState(pages, options = {}){ const previousSelected = options.keepSelected ? state.selectedMonthId : ""; const previousYear = options.keepSelected ? state.selectedYear : ""; if(options.reset !== false) resetDataState(); const normalized = normalizeFinanceDataset(pages); state.rawPages = normalized.pages;
