state.selectedYear = chooseDefaultYear(years, normalized.months, previousYear, previousSelected); const scopedMonths = normalized.months.filter(month => !state.selectedYear || getMonthYear(month) === state.selectedYear); state.selectedMonthId = scopedMonths.some(month => month.id === previousSelected) ? previousSelected : scopedMonths[scopedMonths.length - 1]?.id || normalized.months[normalized.months.length - 1]?.id || ""; state.isGlobal = false; if(options.source) state.source = options.source; return normalized; } function calculateDashboardMetrics(months){ const list = (months || []).filter(Boolean); // Une cellule absente ne repr√©sente pas un z√©ro : seuls les mois o√π un salaire // est r√©ellement renseign√© entrent dans la moyenne. Un z√©ro explicite // reste toutefois une valeur valide gr√¢ce au marqueur hasIncome. const salaryMonths = list.filter(month => month.hasIncome === true || (month.hasIncome == null && Number(month.salary) !== 0)); const expenseMonths = list.filter(month => month.hasExpenses === true || (month.hasExpenses == null && Number(month.expenses) !== 0)); const salaryValues = salaryMonths.map(month => Number(month.salary || 0)); const savingsValues = list.map(month => Number(month.savingsTotal || 0)); return { monthCount: list.length, incomeMonthCount: salaryMonths.length, avgSalary: salaryValues.length ? avg(salaryValues) : null, totalSalary: sum(list.map(month => Number(month.salary || 0))), totalSavings: sum(savingsValues), maxExpenseMonth: maxBy(expenseMonths, month => Math.abs(Number(month.expenses || 0))), minExpenseMonth: minBy(expenseMonths, month => Math.abs(Number(month.expenses || 0))) }; } function getDashboardSharingSnapshot(){ // Version d√©riv√©e destin√©e aux amis : elle ne contient ni cellules brutes // ni formules, uniquement les montants d√©j√† affich√©s dans le dashboard. return { v:"dashboard-1", updatedAt:Date.now(), months:(state.months||[]).map(month=>({ label:month.label, year:getMonthYear(month), salary:Number(month.salary||0), expenses:Number(month.expenses||0), savingsTotal:Number(month.savingsTotal||0), hasIncome:month.hasIncome===true, hasExpenses:month.hasExpenses===true, hasSavings:month.hasSavings===true, ruleBreakdown:(month.ruleBreakdown||[]).map(item=>({name:item.name,value:Number(item.value||0)})) })) }; } window.getDashboardSharingSnapshot = getDashboardSharingSnapshot; function updateSummaryCards(){ const metrics = calculateDashboardMetrics(getVisibleMonths()); if(!metrics.monthCount){ elements.avgSalary.textContent = "-"; elements.totalSavings.textContent = "-"; elements.maxExpenseMonth.textContent = "-"; elements.maxExpenseValue.textContent = "-"; elements.minExpenseMonth.textContent = "-"; elements.minExpenseValue.textContent = "-"; elements.avgSalaryNote.textContent = "Tous les mois"; elements.totalSavingsNote.textContent = "Comptes cumul√©s"; var sgc = document.getElementById("savingsGoalCard"); if (sgc) sgc.hidden = true; return; } elements.avgSalary.textContent = metrics.avgSalary === null ? "-" : formatEuro(metrics.avgSalary); elements.avgSalaryNote.textContent = metrics.incomeMonthCount ? metrics.incomeMonthCount + " mois avec salaire" : "Aucun salaire renseign√©"; elements.totalSavings.textContent = formatEuro(Math.abs(metrics.totalSavings)); elements.totalSavingsNote.textContent = formatPercent(Math.abs(metrics.totalSavings), metrics.totalSalary) + " du salaire total"; elements.maxExpenseMonth.textContent = metrics.maxExpenseMonth?.label || "-"; elements.maxExpenseValue.textContent = metrics.maxExpenseMonth ? formatEuro(Math.abs(metrics.maxExpenseMonth.expenses)) : "-"; elements.minExpenseMonth.textContent = metrics.minExpenseMonth?.label || "-"; elements.minExpenseValue.textContent = metrics.minExpenseMonth ? formatEuro(Math.abs(metrics.minExpenseMonth.expenses)) : "-"; // La barre de progression de l'objectif d'√©pargne refl√®te UNIQUEMENT le mois s√©lectionn√© const selectedMonth = getSelectedMonth(); const selectedSavings = selectedMonth ? Math.abs(Number(selectedMonth.savingsTotal || 0)) : 0; updateSavingsGoalCard(selectedSavings); } function updateSavingsGoalCard(currentSavings) { var card = document.getElementById("savingsGoalCard"); if (!card) return; var profile = null; try { profile = JSON.parse(safeStore.getItem("personalFinanceDashboard.setupProfile") || "null"); } catch (e) {} var goal = parseFloat(profile && profile.savingsGoal); if (!goal || goal <= 0) { card.hidden = true; return; } card.hidden = false; var current = Math.max(0, currentSavings || 0); var pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((current / goal) * 100)); var remaining = Math.max(0, goal - current); var valEl = document.getElementById("savingsGoalValue"); var pctEl = document.getElementById("savingsGoalProgressText"); var fillEl = document.getElementById("savingsGoalBarFill"); var curEl = document.getElementById("savingsGoalCurrent"); var remEl = document.getElementById("savingsGoalRemaining"); if (valEl) valEl.textContent = formatEuro(goal); if (pctEl) pctEl.textContent = pct + "%"; if (fillEl) fillEl.style.width = pct + "%"; if (curEl) curEl.textContent = "√âpargn√© : " + formatEuro(current); if (remEl) remEl.textContent = remaining > 0 ? "Restant : " + formatEuro(remaining) : "Objectif atteint !"; card.classList.toggle("goal-reached", current >= goal); } function rebuildDashboardFromCurrentPages(){ if(!state.rawPages.length) return; applyFinancePagesToState(state.rawPages, { reset: false, keepSelected: true }); populateMonthSelect(); updateInterface(); } function toggleServerControl(){ if(!elements.serverControl || elements.serverControl?.classList.contains("is-stopped")) return; const open = !elements.serverControl.classList.contains("is-open"); elements.serverControl.classList.toggle("is-open", open); elements.serverControlButton?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", String(open)); } async function requestServerShutdown(){ if(!elements.serverControl || elements.serverControl.classList.contains("is-stopped")) return; elements.serverControl.classList.add("is-open"); elements.serverControlButton?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "true"); if(elements.stopServerButton){ elements.stopServerButton.disabled = true; elements.stopServerButton.textContent = "Arr√™t..."; } try{ await fetch(buildLocalApiUrl("/api/server/shutdown"), { method: "POST", cache: "no-store" }); }catch{} elements.serverControl.classList.remove("is-open"); elements.serverControl.classList.add("is-stopped"); if(elements.stopServerButton){ elements.stopServerButton.textContent = "Relance .bat"; elements.stopServerButton.disabled = true; } showMessage("Serveur arr√™t√©. Relance start-dashboard.bat pour rouvrir le dashboard.", 22000); } function emitSiteEvent(type, message, details = {}){ try{ if(location.protocol === "file:" || typeof fetch !== "function") return; fetch(buildLocalApiUrl("/api/server/log"), { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify({ type, message, details }), keepalive: true }).catch(() => {}); }catch{} } // Pont tableur -> dashboard : construit directement des "pages" a partir // des lignes/colonnes du tableur (ligne 0 = en-tetes, lignes suivantes = donnees), // puis reutilise le moteur de months/categories existant (applyFinancePagesToState). function wrapSheetValue(val){ if(val instanceof Date) return { type: "date", date: { start: val.toISOString() } }; if(typeof val === "number" && isFinite(val)) return { type: "number", number: val }; return { type: "rich_text", rich_text: [{ type: "text", text: String(val), plain_text: String(val) }] }; } function syncDashboardFromFinanceSheet(opts){ opts = opts || {}; var silent = opts.silent === true; var btn = document.getElementById("syncSheetButton"); try{ if(typeof window.FinanceSheet === "undefined" || typeof window.FinanceSheet.buildWorkbook !== "function"){ if(!silent) showMessage("Le tableur n'est pas encore charg√©. Ouvre l'onglet Tableur d'abord."); return false; } // Guard: si le tableur est vide, vide aussi le dashboard var snap = window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot ? window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot() : null; var hasData = snap && snap.sheets && snap.sheets.some(function(sh){ return sh.cells && Object.keys(sh.cells).length > 0; }); if(!hasData){ resetDataState(); updateInterface(); return false; } if(typeof window.XLSX === "undefined" || !window.XLSX?.utils?.sheet_to_json){ if(!silent) showMessage("La biblioth√®que Excel n'est pas charg√©e. V√©rifie ta connexion internet."); return false; } const wb = window.FinanceSheet.buildWorkbook(); if(!wb || !wb.SheetNames || !wb.SheetNames.length){ applyFinancePagesToState([], { source: "tableur", reset: true }); populateMonthSelect(); updateInterface(); if(!silent) showMessage("Le tableur est vide. Saisis des donn√©es dans l'onglet Tableur puis clique sur Recharger."); return false; } const pages = []; wb.SheetNames.forEach((sheetName) => { const ws = wb.Sheets[sheetName]; if(!ws || !ws["!ref"]) return; const aoa = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(ws, { header: 1, raw: true, blankrows: false }); if(!aoa || aoa.length < 2) return; const headers = aoa[0] || []; // D√©tection des en-t√™tes qui sont des mois (layout "mois en colonnes") const monthCols = []; for(let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++){ if(parseFrenchMonth(headers[c])) monthCols.push(c); } const isMatrix = monthCols.length >= 2 && !parseFrenchMonth(headers[0]); if(isMatrix){ // Chaque colonne-mois devient une page ; les lignes sont les cat√©gories. const labelCol = 0; monthCols.forEach(c => { const props = {}; let nonEmpty = 0; for(let r = 1; r < aoa.length; r++){ const row = aoa[r]; if(!row) continue; const label = row[labelCol]; const val = row[c]; if(label === undefined || label === null || label === "") continue; if(val === undefined || val === null || val === "") continue; props[String(label)] = wrapSheetValue(val); nonEmpty++; } if(nonEmpty){ // Extraire l'ann√©e du nom de feuille (ex: "2025", "2025 - Table 1", "2026 - Budget") var yearExtracted = (function(){ var m = String(sheetName).match(/(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})/); return m ? m[1] : null; })(); // Construire une date Mois compl√®te si on a l'ann√©e if(yearExtracted){ var monthDate = parseFrenchMonth(headers[c] + " " + yearExtracted); if(monthDate){ props["Mois"] = { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(monthDate) } }; } else { props["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(headers[c]); } props["Ann√©e"] = wrapSheetValue(yearExtracted); } else { props["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(headers[c]); } pages.push({ id: sheetName + "_m" + c, properties: props }); } }); return; } // === Layout transactionnel : d√©tection automatique === // Chaque ligne est une transaction avec une description et un montant. // On groupe par description et on somme les montants. var isDefaultHeaders = headers.every(function(h){ return !h || /^[A-Z]{1,3}$/.test(String(h)); }); var hasMonthHeader = headers.some(function(h){ return h && /mois/i.test(String(h)); }); if(!isMatrix && (isDefaultHeaders || !hasMonthHeader)){ // Analyser les colonnes pour trouver la description (texte) et le montant (num√©rique) var textCol = -1, numCol = -1, dateCol = -1; var textScore = 0, numScore = 0, dateScore = 0; for(var c = 0; c < headers.length; c++){ var texts = 0, nums = 0, dates = 0; for(var r = 1; r < aoa.length; r++){ var rv = aoa[r] && aoa[r][c]; if(rv === undefined || rv === null || rv === "") continue; var rvStr = String(rv).trim(); // Nombre ? if(typeof rv === "number" || /^-?[\d\s.,]+‚Ç¨?$/.test(rvStr)){ nums++; } // Date ? else if(/^(0?[1-9]|[12][0-9]|3[01])[\/\-.](0?[1-9]|1[0-2])[\/\-.]\d{2,4}$/.test(rvStr) || rv instanceof Date){ dates++; } // Texte (description) else if(rvStr.length >= 2 && !/^[A-Z]{1,3}$/.test(rvStr)){ texts++; } } if(dates > dateScore){ dateCol = c; dateScore = dates; } if(nums > numScore){ numCol = c; numScore = nums; } if(texts > textScore){ textCol = c; textScore = texts; } } // Transaction-style : au moins 2 lignes avec description + montant if(textCol >= 0 && numCol >= 0 && textCol !== numCol && textScore >= 2 && numScore >= 2){ // Grouper par description, sommer les montants var grouped = {}; var monthFromTransactions = null; for(var r = 1; r < aoa.length; r++){ var tRow = aoa[r]; if(!tRow) continue; var desc = tRow[textCol]; var amt = tRow[numCol]; if(desc === undefined || desc === null || desc === "") continue; var descStr = String(desc).trim(); if(amt === undefined || amt === null || amt === "") continue; var amtNum = typeof amt === "number" ? amt : parseFloat(String(amt).replace(/[^\d.,-]/g, "").replace(",", ".")) || 0; if(!grouped[descStr]) grouped[descStr] = 0; grouped[descStr] += amtNum; // D√©tecter le mois depuis la colonne date if(dateCol >= 0 && !monthFromTransactions){ var dv = tRow[dateCol]; if(dv){ monthFromTransactions = parseFrenchMonth(String(dv)) || monthFromTransactions; } } } var tProps = {}; var tNonEmpty = 0; var tKeys = Object.keys(grouped); tKeys.forEach(function(k){ // Classification automatique bas√©e sur le signe et les mots-cl√©s var totalAmt = grouped[k]; var autoRule = classifyFinancialProperty(k); if(!autoRule || autoRule === "Ignorer"){ // Si pas reconnu par mots-cl√©s, utiliser le signe du montant autoRule = totalAmt < 0 ? "D√©pense" : "Salaire"; } tProps[k] = { type: "number", number: totalAmt, _autoRule: autoRule, _autoConfidence: 0.6 }; tNonEmpty++; }); if(monthFromTransactions){ tProps["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(monthFromTransactions); } else if(dateCol >= 0){ // Utiliser la premi√®re date comme mois var firstDate = aoa[1] && aoa[1][dateCol]; if(firstDate){ var fm = parseFrenchMonth(String(firstDate)); if(fm) tProps["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(fm); } } if(tNonEmpty){ var yearMatchTx = String(sheetName).match(/(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})/); if(yearMatchTx) tProps["Ann√©e"] = wrapSheetValue(yearMatchTx[1]); pages.push({ id: sheetName + "_transactions", properties: tProps }); } return; // Passer √† la feuille suivante } } // Layout synth√®se : les lignes sont des mois. On d√©tecte la colonne "Mois". let moisCol = headers.findIndex(h => h && /mois/i.test(String(h))); if(moisCol < 0){ for(let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++){ let checked = 0, months = 0; for(let r = 1; r < Math.min(aoa.length, 6); r++){ const v = aoa[r]?.[c]; if(v === undefined || v === null || v === "") continue; checked++; if(parseFrenchMonth(v)) months++; } if(checked >= 2 && months === checked){ moisCol = c; break; } } } for(let r = 1; r < aoa.length; r++){ const row = aoa[r]; if(!row) continue; const props = {}; let nonEmpty = 0, moisVal = null; for(let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++){ const key = headers[c]; if(!key) continue; const val = row[c]; if(val === undefined || val === null || val === "") continue; if(c === moisCol){ moisVal = val; continue; } props[String(key)] = wrapSheetValue(val); nonEmpty++; } if(moisCol >= 0 && moisVal !== null && moisVal !== undefined){ // Extraire l'ann√©e du nom de feuille pour cr√©er une date compl√®te var yearExtractedSyn = (function(){ var m = String(sheetName).match(/(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})/); return m ? m[1] : null; })(); if(yearExtractedSyn){ var md = parseFrenchMonth(String(moisVal) + " " + yearExtractedSyn); if(md){ props["Mois"] = { type: "date", date: { start: formatDateIsoLocal(md) } }; } else { props["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(moisVal); } props["Ann√©e"] = wrapSheetValue(yearExtractedSyn); } else { props["Mois"] = wrapSheetValue(moisVal); } nonEmpty++; } // Si le nom de la feuille contient une ann√©e, l'ajouter comme propri√©t√© Ann√©e var yearMatchSyn = String(sheetName).match(/(19\d{2}|20\d{2}|21\d{2})/); if(yearMatchSyn && !props["Ann√©e"]){ props["Ann√©e"] = wrapSheetValue(yearMatchSyn[1]); nonEmpty++; } if(nonEmpty) pages.push({ id: sheetName + "_" + r, properties: props }); } }); applyFinancePagesToState(pages, { source: "tableur", reset: true }); populateMonthSelect(); updateInterface(); if(pages.length) setConnection(true, "Tableur"); if(!silent){ var catCount = (state.detectedProperties||[]).length; var monthCount = (state.months||[]).length; if(monthCount === 0){ showMessage("Le tableur ne contient pas encore de donn√©es. Saisis tes donn√©es dans l'onglet Tableur puis clique sur Recharger.", 8000); } else { showMessage("Donn√©es recharg√©es : " + monthCount + " mois, " + catCount + " cat√©gories d√©tect√©es.", 5000); } } return true; }catch(err){ console.error("syncDashboardFromFinanceSheet:", err); if(!silent) showMessage("Erreur lors du rechargement : " + (err.message||err), 10000); return false; } finally { try{ if(typeof window.updateSheetStatus === "function") window.updateSheetStatus(); }catch(e){} } } window.syncDashboardFromFinanceSheet = syncDashboardFromFinanceSheet; window.populateYearSelect = populateYearSelect; // === Indicateur de statut du tableur === function updateSheetStatus(){ var dot = document.getElementById("sheetStatusDot"); if(!dot) return; var hasData = false; try{ if(typeof window.FinanceSheet !== "undefined" && window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot){ var snap = window.FinanceSheet.getSnapshot(); hasData = snap && snap.sheets && snap.sheets.some(function(sh){ return sh.cells && Object.keys(sh.cells).length > 0; }); } }catch(e){} dot.className = "sheet-status-dot"; if(!hasData){ dot.classList.add("not-loaded"); } else if(navigator.onLine){ dot.classList.add("online"); } else { dot.classList.add("connected-offline"); } } window.updateSheetStatus = updateSheetStatus; // Update on load and when online/offline changes window.addEventListener("online", updateSheetStatus); window.addEventListener("offline", updateSheetStatus); document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", updateSheetStatus); setInterval(updateSheetStatus, 5000); document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", ()=>{ const b=document.getElementById("syncSheetButton"); if(b) b.addEventListener("click", ()=>window.GFUI.withBusy(b,()=>window.syncDashboardFromFinanceSheet())); }); async function importFinanceFile(file){ if(!file) return; const extension = (file.name.split(".").pop() || "").toLowerCase(); const textLike = ["csv", "tsv", "txt", "json", "xml", "html", "htm"].includes(extension); const spreadsheetLike = ["xlsx", "xls", "xlsm", "ods"].includes(extension); if(!spreadsheetLike && !textLike){ showMessage("Format non pris en charge. Utilise un fichier Excel, CSV, JSON, XML ou HTML contenant un tableau."); return; } setLoading(true); try{ let tables = [], workbook = null, decodeIssues = [], encoding = ""; if(spreadsheetLike){ if(typeof window.XLSX === "undefined" || !window.XLSX?.read || !window.XLSX?.utils?.sheet_to_json){ showMessage("Le module Excel n'a pas pu √™tre charg√©. V√©rifie la connexion internet."); return; } const buffer = await readFileArrayBuffer(file); try{ workbook = XLSX.read(buffer, { type: "array", cellDates: true, raw: true, WTF: false }); }catch(err){ throw new Error("Structure Excel invalide ou fichier corrompu : " + (err?.message || err)); } tables = tableFromXlsxWorkbook(workbook); }else{ const buffer = await readFileArrayBuffer(file); const decoded = decodeTextBuffer(buffer, file.name); decodeIssues = decoded.issues || []; encoding = decoded.encoding || ""; file._text = decoded.text; file._analysisIssues = decodeIssues; file._textEncoding = encoding; tables = tableFromTextFile(file.name, decoded.text, decodeIssues); } if(!tables.length) throw new Error("Aucune structure tabulaire exploitable n'a √©t√© d√©tect√©e dans ce fichier."); const analysisBundle = analyzeWorkbookSheets(tables, { fileName: file.name, sheetName: tables[0]?.name || "Feuille 1", issues: decodeIssues }); const analysis = analysisBundle.chosen; if(!analysis) throw new Error("Impossible d'analyser ce fichier."); if(!analysis.dataRows?.length) throw new Error("Le fichier ne contient pas assez de lignes exploitables."); const workbookPages = buildPagesFromWorkbookBundle(analysisBundle, file.name); const pages = workbookPages.pages; if(!pages.length) throw new Error("Aucune ligne exploitable trouv√©e dans le fichier."); const normalized = applyFinancePagesToState(pages, { source: "file", reset: true }); const months = normalized.months; const reviewCount = (normalized.categoryModel.detectedProperties || []).filter(prop => prop.needsReview).length; const reviewCategories = (normalized.categoryModel.detectedProperties || []).filter(prop => prop.needsReview).map(prop => prop.name); const issues = workbookPages.issues || []; const warningCount = issues.filter(issue => issue.severity !== "info").length; const importedSheets = workbookPages.importedSheets.length ? workbookPages.importedSheets : [analysis.sheetName || tables[0]?.name || "Feuille 1"]; state.importedFile = { fileName: file.name, sheetName: importedSheets.join(", "), importedSheets, sheetCount: importedSheets.length, rows: pages.length, monthCount: months.length, processedRows: workbookPages.processedRows, cols: workbookPages.maxCols || analysis.cols || 0, fileType: extension, sourceKind: analysis.sourceKind || "table", score: analysis.score || 0, issuesCount: warningCount, reviewCount, reviewCategories, issues, encoding, importMode: workbookPages.importMode }; state.importAnalysis = { fileName: file.name, fileType: extension, sourceKind: analysis.sourceKind || "table", sheetName: importedSheets.join(", "), importedSheets, sheetCount: importedSheets.length, rows: workbookPages.processedRows || analysis.rows || 0, monthCount: months.length, cols: workbookPages.maxCols || analysis.cols || 0, score: analysis.score || 0, columns: analysis.columns || [], sheetSummaries: analysisBundle.sheetSummaries || [], issues, encoding, importMode: workbookPages.importMode, reviewCount }; logImportIssues(file.name, issues); renderImportBadge(state.importedFile); renderImportAnalysis(state.importAnalysis); populateMonthSelect(); setConnection(true, "Fichier import√©"); updateInterface(); setTimeout(function(){ if(typeof window.populateYearSelect === "function") window.populateYearSelect(); }, 500); emitSiteEvent("import", "Import fichier r√©ussi", { fileName: file.name, sheets: importedSheets.length, rows: pages.length, months: months.length }); if(months.length === 0) showMessage("Le fichier a √©t√© analys√©, mais aucun champ financier clair n'a √©t√© d√©tect√©."); else if(reviewCount) showMessage("Import r√©ussi : " + file.name + ". " + reviewCount + " categorie" + (reviewCount > 1 ? "s" : "") + " √† confirmer dans les r√©glages.", 22000); else if(warningCount) showMessage("Import r√©ussi : " + file.name + " (" + warningCount + " anomalie" + (warningCount > 1 ? "s" : "") + " consign√©e" + (warningCount > 1 ? "s" : "") + ").", 20000); else showMessage("Import r√©ussi : " + file.name); }catch(err){ console.error(err); renderImportAnalysis(null); emitSiteEvent("error", "Import fichier √©chou√©", { reason: err?.message || "Erreur inconnue" }); showMessage(err?.message || "Impossible d'importer ce fichier."); }finally{ setLoading(false); } } function runFileAnalysisSelfTest(){ const fixtures = [ { name: "ledger-edge-cases.csv", text: " Date ; Libelle ; Montant \n05/06/2024 ; Cafe & The ; -12,50\n\n31-07-2024 ; Salaire ; 2 500,00\nligne corrompue ; ??? ; abc\n15 aout 2024 ; Epargne Livret ; 100,00", minPages: 3, expectsIssues: true }, { name: "summary-duplicates.csv", text: "Mois;Salaire;Depense;Depense\n2024-01-01;2500;100;50\nFevrier 2024;2600;120;60", minPages: 2, expectedMode: "summary", expectsIssues: true }, { name: "matrix-budget-2026.csv", text: ";Cat\u00e9gorie / Poste;Janvier;F\u00e9vrier;Mars;TOTAL\n;\ud83d\udcb0 Revenus;;;;\n; Salaire;1538;1519;1519;4576\n;Sous-total Revenus;1538;1519;1519;4576\n;\ud83d\udcb8 D\u00e9penses;;;;\n; D\u00e9penses;300;300;300;900\n; Loyer;0;0;0;0\n;Sous-total D\u00e9penses;300;300;300;900\n;\ud83c\udfe6 \u00c9pargne;;;;\n; Livret A;1000;1000;1000;3000\n; \u00c9pargne (Revolut);0;10;5;15\n;Sous-total \u00c9pargne;1000;1010;1005;3015", minPages: 3, expectedMode: "matrix", expectsIssues: false } ]; const results = fixtures.map(fixture => { const tables = tableFromTextFile(fixture.name, fixture.text, []); const bundle = analyzeWorkbookSheets(tables, { fileName: fixture.name, sheetName: tables[0]?.name || "CSV" }); const analysis = bundle.chosen; const pages = buildPagesFromAnalysis(analysis, fixture.name, analysis?.sheetName || "CSV"); const ok = pages.length >= fixture.minPages && (!fixture.expectedMode || analysis.importMode === fixture.expectedMode) && (!fixture.expectsIssues || (analysis.issues || []).length > 0); return { name: fixture.name, ok, pages: pages.length, issues: (analysis.issues || []).length, mode: analysis.importMode }; }); return { ok: results.every(result => result.ok), results }; } window.__fileAnalysisSelfTest = runFileAnalysisSelfTest; // FILE_ANALYSIS_ENGINE_END function init(){ restoreAppearance(); restoreConfiguration(); setActiveSource("tableur"); renderImportBadge(null); createCustomDropdown(elements.yearSelect); createCustomDropdown(elements.viewSelect); createCustomDropdown(elements.monthSelect); wireEvents(); renderCustomRulesList(); updateInterface(); // Re-populate yearSelect apr√®s que FinanceSheet soit initialis√© setTimeout(function(){ try{ populateYearSelect(); }catch(e){} }, 600); setTimeout(showLatestUpdate,1800); } init();
